Model architecture: 摘要未说明。不涉及深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。文中提到"low communication overhead"和"scalable and real-time"，但这是控制方法的通信效率，与模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。文中提到"real-time"和"low communication overhead"，但这是针对多机器人控制系统的实时性，而非星上推理的延迟、参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。通过仿真和实验验证有效性，但未提及具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 通过大量仿真和实验验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性，并在某些情况下证明了理论最优到达时间。但未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。摘要本身未说明方法的局限性。

Ideas for my research: 与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接联系是：如果未来研究涉及无人机集群协同遥感（如多无人机协同目标检测），该文的分布式一致性控制方法可能对协同到达有参考价值，但与模型轻量化无关。

Abstract: The simultaneous arrival of multiple mobile robots at a target point is crucial for cooperation tasks such as cooperative encirclement, disaster relief, and environmental monitoring. Although the simultaneous arrival problem itself is already complex, the problem becomes more challenging when there are constraints on the robot trajectory curvatures and the speeds are required to be constant (possibly different for different robots), and the control law for robots needs to be distributed. These constraints are typical for a multi-robot system consisting of, e.g., fixed-wing UAVs. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a distributed switching control method based on the maximum consensus protocol. By exploiting the geometric properties of Dubins paths along with optimization principles, a virtual time variable is introduced, and a hybrid control law that combines optimal control with saturated proportional control is designed. Under the proposed control law, each robot is driven to approach the maximum virtual time among its neighbors, thereby achieving simultaneous arrival under some mild conditions. Furthermore, we prove that in certain cases the proposed method attains a theoretically optimal arrival time. The approach is scalable and real-time, with low communication overhead. Its effectiveness and robustness are validated through extensive simulations and experiments.

</details>


### [123] [Interventional Causal Circuits for Safe Robot Action Testing and Failure Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14826)
*Naren Vasantakumaar,Tom Schierenbeck,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于因果推理的机器人动作安全测试框架，通过联合概率树和因果电路实现拒绝动作的因果诊断与修正，无需重训练或额外数据采集。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人动作安全测试与因果推理，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人动作安全测试与因果诊断；场景：ROS2仿真环境中的机器人运动规划；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: Joint Probability Tree (JPT) + Causal Circuit (基于Marginal-Deterministic Variable Tree构建)，用于因果推理和动作参数修正。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验环境：ROS2仿真环境；评价指标：失败尝试减少比例（high-quality JPT下减少10.3%，degraded JPT下减少37%）。

Experiments: 在ROS2仿真环境中验证，Causal Circuit在高质量JPT下减少失败尝试10.3%，在退化JPT下减少37%。每次拒绝计划生成结构化因果报告。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文主题为机器人安全动作的因果推理，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交集。

Abstract: Safe physical AI for robot actions are required not only likely to succeed but tested to be safe before execution. In practice, however, formal testing of motion parameters is computationally expensive, and the cost scales poorly with the dimensionality of the action space. When a proposed action is rejected by a tester, the naive response is to resample blindly until a passing candidate is found. This is wasteful, uninformative, and offers no convergence. We argue that rejection should instead trigger causal diagnosis: a principled identification of which action parameter caused the failure and what corrective value maximises the probability of passing testing under the interventional probability distribution. We propose a closed-loop framework that couples a Joint Probability Tree (JPT) with a Causal Circuit derived from a Marginal-Deterministic Variable Tree, enabling exact polytime computation without retraining, or additional data collection. The framework validates tractability of all interventional queries before the robot begins operating, and out-of-support candidates are detected and excluded from correction automatically. We perform experiments in a ROS2 simulation environment, and the framework demonstrates complementary roles across quality of distribution: under a high-quality JPT, the Causal Circuit reduces failed attempts by 10.3% and under a degraded JPT, it reduces total failed attempts by 37%. Every rejected plan produces a structured, interpretable causal report naming the primary cause variable, its observed value, and the recommended corrective region, supporting operator oversight and autonomous recovery without a separately trained failure model.

</details>


### [124] [KineFuse: Kinematic-Aware Haptic Fusion for In-Hand Occluded-Object Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14842)
*Chanyoung Ahn,Jaesung Lee,Sungwoo Park,Donghyun Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种运动学感知的手指级编码器，利用稀疏触觉信号（本体感知、力/力矩、接触信号）辅助预训练视觉6D位姿跟踪器，解决灵巧手操作中手指遮挡导致的跟踪失败问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是灵巧手在操作过程中的6D位姿跟踪，属于机器人操作领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：6D位姿跟踪（6D pose tracking）；场景：灵巧手在操作（in-hand manipulation）；传感器：视觉相机 + 触觉传感器（本体感知、力/力矩、二进制接触）；目标类别：被操作物体（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 提出Kinematic-aware Finger-level Encoder（运动学感知的手指级编码器），采用紧凑的手指级tokenization（4个token），与预训练视觉位姿跟踪器融合。包含跨模态门控机制（cross-modal gating），视觉专用于平移估计，一个注意力头专用于触觉旋转估计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向机器人操作，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 评估分为三个层次：逐帧修正（per-frame refinement）、序列开环跟踪（sequential open-loop tracking）、闭环操作（closed-loop manipulation）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。指标包括跟踪成功率、下游重定向任务成功率。

Experiments: 主要发现：(i) 逐帧评估无法区分编码器质量，序列跟踪将架构差异放大至15倍；(ii) 结构化编码器学习任务特定跨模态门控，视觉专用于平移，一个注意力头专用于触觉旋转；(iii) 4 token的紧凑手指级表示优于扁平融合和关节级表示。下游重定向任务中改进的跟踪带来更高成功率。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性分析。摘要本身未说明方法的局限性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可能有间接启发：(1) 多模态融合中的跨模态门控机制（cross-modal gating）思路，可类比遥感中光学与SAR/高光谱的多模态融合；(2) 紧凑token化表示（compact tokenization）的思想，对遥感目标检测中的特征压缩有参考价值；(3) 序列级评估比逐帧评估更能反映架构差异，对遥感视频流目标检测的评估策略有启发。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires continuous 6D pose tracking, yet the manipulating fingers inevitably occlude the object from the camera. We study how to structure the sparse haptic signals already available on multi-fingered hands, including proprioception, proximal force/torque, and binary contact, to complement a pretrained visual pose tracker under occlusion. We propose a kinematic-aware finger-level encoder and systematically compare it against four alternative designs through three levels of evaluation: per-frame refinement, sequential open-loop tracking, and closed-loop manipulation. Our experiments reveal that (i) per-frame evaluation cannot distinguish encoder quality, while sequential tracking amplifies architectural differences by up to 15 times; (ii) the structured encoder learns task-specific cross-modal gating, using vision exclusively for translation and dedicating one attention head to haptics for rotation, without explicit supervision; and (iii) compact finger-level tokenization with 4 tokens outperforms both flat fusion and joint-level representations, which suppress vision through norm dominance. We validate that improved tracking yields higher success in a downstream reorientation task and provide qualitative real-world demonstrations. Our project page is available at https://cold-young.github.io/kine-fuse/.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Human-like Physical Intelligence: LifelongVision-Language-Action Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14852)
*Yao He,Gan Sun,Wenqi Liang,Fazeng Li,Yang Cong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向机器人操作的双时间尺度终身VLA学习框架LifelongVLA，通过LoRA门控模块和缓存高效回放策略缓解塑性-稳定性权衡问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人操作（robotic manipulation）领域的Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型终身学习，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及星上/边缘部署的轻量化问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非课题涉及机器人操作或VLA模型的终身学习，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作中的终身学习（lifelong learning）；应用场景：开放世界机器人部署（xArm机器人）；传感器类型：视觉（Vision）+语言（Language）；目标类别：机器人操作技能/行为。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 基于VLA（Vision-Language-Action）模型，提出双时间尺度LoRA门控模块（dual-timescale LoRA gating module），包含短期适配器（short-term adapter）和长期适配器（long-term adapter），通过任务感知门（task-aware gate）集成。

Lightweight method: 涉及LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）轻量微调方法，以及缓存高效随机回放策略（cache-efficient stochastic replay strategy）以减少存储开销。但这是针对机器人终身学习的轻量化，而非遥感模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"low-cost robotic deployment"和"reduced reliance on retraining"，但未给出具体数值。与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅在实验中提到"outperforms existing baselines"和"efficient skill expansion, robust retention"等定性描述。

Experiments: 在xArm机器人上进行真实世界部署实验，对比现有baseline方法，在技能扩展（skill expansion）和旧技能保持（retention）上表现更优。但无定量指标对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供任何参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等量化指标；3. 仅在机器人操作场景验证，未在遥感/视觉检测任务上评估；4. 终身学习设定与遥感在轨模型持续学习有一定概念交叉，但任务本质不同。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感无关，但以下思路可能有间接借鉴价值：1. 双时间尺度适配机制（dual-timescale adaptation）的思想可用于遥感在轨模型的持续学习场景，即在轨模型需要适应新类别（短期塑性）同时保持旧类别检测能力（长期稳定）；2. LoRA轻量微调方法可用于遥感基础模型的参数高效微调；3. 缓存高效回放策略可借鉴用于在轨数据存储受限场景下的增量学习。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Similar to the natural capabilities of humans to sequentially learn new tasks, robots with Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models should possess lifelong learning ability to learn a new task when deployed in open-world environments. However, most recently proposed lifelong learning models aim to effectively learn the current task (plasticity) or maintain high accuracy on previous tasks (stability), while the plasticity-stability trade-off remains largely unsolved in robotic manipulation models. To address this fundamental challenge, we propose a cache-efficient lifelong Vision-Language-Action learning framework for robotic manipulation (i.e., LifelongVLA), which alleviates the plasticity-stability trade-off with a dual-timescale adaptation mechanism while achieving low-cost robotic deployment with a cache-efficient replay strategy. More concretely, we propose a dual-timescale LoRA gating module to decompose VLA adaptation into two lightweight pathways: a short-term adapter for plasticity and a long-term adapter for stable consolidation. These pathways are integrated via a task-aware gate, enabling explicit control of the plasticity-stability trade-off. In the skill replay phase, a cache-efficient stochastic replay strategy is proposed to preserve more balanced retention signals without full-trajectory storage. Finally, experiments show that LifelongVLA outperforms existing baselines, demonstrating efficient skill expansion, robust retention of learned manipulation behaviors, and reduced reliance on retraining for real-world deployment on an xArm robot.

</details>


### [126] [Modeling and Validation of Quality of Control for Edge-Offloaded Collaborative Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14853)
*Neelabhro Roy,Mikael Hammarling,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Gourav Prateek Sharma,Mani H. Dhullipalla,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,James Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究复杂环境下协作控制的通信-控制协同设计，利用5G测试床分析随机无线延迟和可靠性对机器人导航、避障及能效的影响，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦机器人协作控制中的无线通信延迟与可靠性问题，属于机器人控制与通信协同领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：协作控制中的导航与动态避障；应用场景：机器人协作环境；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络相关方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验环境：私有5G测试床；评价指标：QoC（quality of control）、延迟、可靠性；数据集：无。

Experiments: 在私有5G测试床上验证了不同延迟、可靠性和控制配置下的QoC性能，对比了ROS 2 QoS策略，RELIABLE QoS比BEST-EFFORT在特定设置下QoC提升51.5%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是机器人协作控制中的通信-控制协同问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Collaborative control in complex environments is severely challenged by stochastic wireless delay and reliability variations, which can degrade navigation, tracking, and collision avoidance. These network-induced uncertainties complicate the maintenance of energy efficiency during collaborative tasks, and can potentially lead to over-provisioning of resources. In this paper, for a navigation setup with dynamic collision avoidance, we address this challenge by expanding the quality of control (QoC) framework from prior works to practical robotic models. Our approach (i) models end-to-end network effects on closed-loop performance, (ii) systematically explores the impact of various control parameters dictating robotic motion on network latency-reliability (iii) validates these models through experiments on a private 5G testbed across varying delay, reliability and control configurations. Our analysis indicates the optimal control-communication co-design operating regimes for practical robots and also compares the QoC performance of standard ROS~2 quality of service (QoS) policies under real-world conditions and showing how RELIABLE QoS offers 51.5% better QoC than BEST-EFFORT under certain experimental settings.

</details>


### [127] [OASIS-Map: Object-Level Change Detection in Multi-Session Mapping using Semantic Correspondence Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14899)
*Haedam Oh,Yifu Tao,Nived Chebrolu,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OASIS-Map，一种基于稠密块级语义对应关系的多会话建图系统，用于半静态环境下的物体级地图时空一致性维护与变化检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人长期巡检中的多会话建图与变化检测，属于机器人/视觉SLAM领域，不涉及遥感卫星、遥感图像、在轨目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多会话物体级建图与变化检测；场景：机器人长期巡检（室内3RScan、停车场、室外市场）；传感器：RGB-D/视觉（摘要未明确说明传感器类型）；目标类别：物体级（object-level），如汽车、室内物体等。

Model architecture: OASIS-Map系统：基于稠密块级语义对应（dense patch-level semantic correspondences）建立时序观测间的关联，实现变化检测与物体增量关联。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：3RScan（室内物体重排）、停车场（视觉相似汽车替换）、室外市场（大规模场景变化）；评价指标：变化检测F1（0.783，停车场场景）、移动物体关联F1（0.667，3RScan）。

Experiments: 在三个真实场景上验证：3RScan物体重排、停车场汽车替换、室外市场场景变化。给出变化检测F1=0.783和物体关联F1=0.667。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；3. 面向机器人地面平台，非星上/空中平台；4. 未报告计算效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但其中"稠密块级语义对应"（dense patch-level semantic correspondences）的思路在遥感时序图像变化检测中可能有参考价值，例如多时相遥感图像中同一目标（如车辆、舰船）的跨时相关联。不过该论文整体与在轨轻量化目标检测方向关联度很低。

Abstract: Map representations which are consistent across repeated visits to a real-world semi-static environment are very useful for long-term robotic inspection. In such settings, the scene may evolve while the robot is absent, with objects appearing, disappearing, moving, or being replaced, quickly making a static map outdated. Existing change-detection methods reason through geometry, category-level semantics, or object persistence. However, achieving reliable object association across revisits remains a key challenge, especially under partial views, occlusion, and imperfect segmentation. In this work, we propose OASIS-Map, a multi-session mapping system that maintains a spatio-temporally consistent object-level map by establishing dense patch-level semantic correspondences between temporal observations. These correspondences detect where the scene has changed and incrementally associate objects across revisits as the robot re-observes the environment. We demonstrate OASIS-Map on three challenging real-world scenarios: object rearrangements in 3RScan, visually similar car replacements in a car park, and large-scale scene changes in an outdoor market. We achieve 0.783 F1 on change detection in a car replacement scenario in a car park and 0.667 F1 on moved object association in 3RScan. https://dynamic.robots.ox.ac.uk/projects/oasis-map/

</details>


### [128] [Human-Robot Interaction in GenAI Architectures via the Agent-Client Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14919)
*Jesus Moncada-Ramirez,Jose-Raul Ruiz-Sarmiento,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Agent-Client Protocol (ACP)的三层解耦架构，用于机器人系统中人机交互层的标准化通信，实现异构用户界面与机器人系统的互操作。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人系统中的人机交互层标准化协议（ACP/MCP），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人系统人机交互架构设计；应用场景：物理移动机器人；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 三层解耦架构：Human Interaction层（用户界面）— Deliberative Orchestration层（智能体编排）— Physical Execution层（物理执行）。接口层使用ACP（Agent-Client Protocol），智能体-执行层使用MCP（Model Context Protocol）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"negligible latency overhead"（可忽略的延迟开销），但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在物理移动机器人上进行，使用三种异构用户界面验证互操作性，评估指标为实时人机协同工作流的可行性及延迟开销。未使用标准遥感/视觉数据集。

Experiments: 在物理移动机器人上验证了三种异构用户界面的互操作性，实现了实时人机协同工作流，延迟开销可忽略。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关。论文讨论的是机器人HRI层通信协议标准化问题，不涉及任何视觉感知、目标检测或模型优化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题域、技术路线和应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI), particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), are driving robotic architectures toward agent-based high-level orchestration, in which natural-language instructions can be translated into context-aware action sequences. While the integration of these agents and robotic capabilities is increasingly converging toward standardization through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), the upper Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) layer remains fragmented by proprietary, ad hoc interfaces that hinder real-time human-in-the-loop collaboration.
  To address this fragmentation, this paper proposes the adoption of the Agent-Client Protocol (ACP) -- a communication standard originally introduced for coding agents in software engineering -- as a unified communication contract for the HRI layer in agent-based robotic systems. By combining ACP at the interface-agent link and MCP at the agent-execution link, we formulate a fully decoupled three-layer architecture that separates human interaction, deliberative orchestration, and physical execution. This topology removes rigid architectural dependencies, enabling heterogeneous user interfaces to connect to the same robotic system and allowing the underlying robotic platform to be replaced without requiring client-specific integration changes. Moreover, it provides native support for collaborative HRI capabilities such as real-time observability, explicit human authorization, and immediate task interruption. We experimentally evaluate the proposed architecture on a physical mobile robot, demonstrating interoperability across three heterogeneous user interfaces and validating real-time human-in-the-loop workflows with negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [129] [Steering Robustness into World Action Models via Mechanistic Interpretability and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14943)
*Jihoon Hong,Julian Skifstad,Qiyue Dai,Alice Chan,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究世界动作模型(WAM)在分布偏移下的鲁棒性问题，利用机械可解释性分析WAM激活空间中的鲁棒性表征，并提出基于对比激活方向的无训练引导方法和WA-LQR最优控制器来提升模型鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人控制领域的World Action Models (WAMs)的鲁棒性和可解释性，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人控制中的鲁棒性分析与引导(steering)；应用场景：机器人操作(robot manipulation)；传感器类型：相机(camera)、夹爪(gripper)；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 研究Cosmos-Policy、DiT4DiT、LingBot-VA等World Action Models (WAMs)架构，分析其激活空间中的线性可分性和局部线性动力学特性。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标包括鲁棒性提升(robustness improvement)，具体针对camera, gripper, visual-noise perturbations的鲁棒性。

Experiments: 在Cosmos-Policy和DiT4DiT模型上，WA-LQR方法相比无引导(unsteered)和提示引导(prompt steering)基线，在相机、夹爪和视觉噪声扰动下提升了鲁棒性。摘要未提供定量数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接借鉴价值；2. 论文聚焦于机器人控制领域的鲁棒性，非视觉检测任务；3. 未讨论模型效率、计算资源约束等部署关键问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：论文中利用激活空间线性可分性进行无训练引导的思路，或许可类比到遥感目标检测模型在分布偏移(如不同传感器、不同分辨率)下的鲁棒性分析，但与本课题核心方向(轻量化、在轨部署)关联极弱。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable semantically- and physically-informed control but are brittle under distribution shift. In this work, we use mechanistic interpretability to study how robustness-relevant perturbations are represented in WAM activation space. Comparing activations across successful and unsuccessful rollouts, we find some WAM architectures exhibit low-dimensional linear separability for robustness-critical features, while others do not. This motivates the use of contrastive activation directions for training-free WAM steering. We also show that local linearity in WAM activation dynamics enables efficient feedback steering via model-based optimal control, yielding World-Action Linear Quadratic Regulator (WA-LQR), a minimally-invasive reduced-order LQR controller. Via mechanistic evaluations, we predict strong steerability in the Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT models but weak steerability in LingBot-VA, consistent with steering intervention results. On Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT, WA-LQR generalizes contrastive directions to new tasks and improves robustness to camera, gripper, and visual-noise perturbations over unsteered and prompt steering baselines.

</details>


### [130] [AeroAct: Action-Centered World-Action Models for Language-Conditioned Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14997)
*Xinhong Zhang,Qiyuan Zhu,Yubo Huang,Haolin Chen,Runqing Wang,Yuhao Mo,Zhongxin Chen,Yu Hu,Xinjiang Wang,Jian Sun,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AeroAct，一种基于动作中心世界-动作模型(WAM)的四旋翼飞行器导航方法，利用预训练视频扩散Transformer预测轨迹-动作块，实现语言条件导航。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于四旋翼飞行器(quadrotor)的语言条件导航，属于无人机低空飞行控制领域，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言条件四旋翼导航(language-conditioned quadrotor navigation)；应用场景：无人机低空飞行、目标跟踪、目标搜索；传感器类型：第一人称视觉相机(egocentric camera)；目标类别：未明确指定具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视频扩散Transformer(video diffusion Transformer)，预测局部轨迹-动作块(local trajectory-action chunks)，输入为第一人称视觉历史、本体感知(proprioception)和语言指令。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到可在物理四旋翼飞行器上执行(executed on a physical quadrotor)，但未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。未涉及星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署技术。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。实验包括闭环仿真(closed-loop simulation)和真实世界实验(real-world experiments)，评估目标跟踪和目标搜索性能。

Experiments: 实验表明时间视觉上下文(temporal visual context)可提升目标跟踪和目标搜索性能，WAM策略可在物理四旋翼上执行。未提供与基线方法的定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 未提供定量指标(参数量、速度、精度等)；4. 方法针对低空无人机飞行控制，不适用于遥感卫星场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是其利用视频扩散Transformer进行时序预测的思路，但遥感卫星在轨目标检测更关注静态图像或视频流中的目标定位与识别，而非飞行控制动作生成。该论文的方法论(世界-动作模型)与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无直接关联。

Abstract: Language-conditioned quadrotor flight requires a policy to ground semantic goals, anticipate the visual consequences of ego-motion, and output control references that remain smooth and dynamically executable under rapidly changing first-person views. Existing aerial vision-language navigation and vision-language-action methods commonly use discrete actions, high-level waypoints, or instantaneous velocity commands, which provide limited supervision about how flight actions change future observations. We present AeroAct, an action-centered world-action model (WAM) for quadrotor navigation. To the best of our knowledge, AeroAct is the first WAM instantiated and demonstrated for real-world aerial flight. The model adapts a pretrained video diffusion Transformer to predict local trajectory-action chunks from egocentric visual history, proprioception, and language. Future first-person frames are used during training as dense consequence supervision, while deployment directly decodes actions without generating future video. To obtain aligned visual, state, language, and dynamically feasible action data, we build a DiffAero-based pipeline with complementary Isaac Lab and 3D Gaussian splatting renderers. We further introduce a low-cost handheld collection device that couples camera observations with motion estimates to recreate flight-like egocentric trajectories, and a self-guidance procedure that improves temporal consistency across overlapping trajectory chunks. Closed-loop simulation and real-world experiments show that temporal visual context improves target tracking and object-search performance, and that WAM-based policies can be executed on a physical quadrotor.

</details>


### [131] [CosFly-VLA: A Spatially Aware Vision-Language-Action Model for UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15004)
*Ruilong Ren,Songsheng Cheng,Yunpeng Zhou,Hanxuan Chen,Xiangyue Wang,Tianle Zeng,Shuai Yuan,Binbo Li,Hanzhong Guo,Ji Pei,Da Zhang,Kangli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CosFly-VLA，一种面向无人机城市动态目标跟踪的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过空间感知预训练、课程微调和闭环强化学习，解决遮挡下的目标丢失问题。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及UAV（无人机）视角下的目标跟踪，与遥感/卫星有一定场景关联，但核心是VLA策略和遮挡下的目标跟踪，不涉及遥感卫星在轨目标检测，也不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。属于泛视觉/机器人控制方法，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分（不推荐精读）。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于UAV目标跟踪+视觉语言动作策略方向，不建议作为本课题的核心参考文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：动态目标跟踪（dynamic target tracking）；场景：城市环境下的UAV跟踪；传感器：UAV摄像头（RGB+深度）；目标类别：通用目标（未指定具体类别如车辆/行人）。不涉及遥感卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱。

Model architecture: CosFly-VLA：基于VLA（Vision-Language-Action）策略的模型，参数量0.8B（8亿参数）。包含空间感知预训练（Spatially Grounded CPT）、多阶段课程SFT、CoT训练、闭环RL优化。多任务预测头：目标定位、可见性估计、连续飞行动作生成。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。模型参数量0.8B，属于较大模型，未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量（除0.8B外）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、边缘/星上部署相关信息。0.8B参数量的VLA模型在星上部署可行性极低。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：500k混合池（用于CPT预训练），自然/长遮挡数据（用于课程SFT）。评价指标：开环ADE（Average Displacement Error）、闭环SR（Success Rate）。未见公开遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）。

Experiments: 对比方法：OpenVLA。结果：CosFly-VLA-0.8B在seen-test上ADE降低34.1%，unseen-test上降低35.3%；闭环优化使SR提升29.8%（seen-test）和2.5%（unseen-test）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 模型参数量0.8B，不适合星上部署；4. 仅针对UAV跟踪场景，不涉及遥感图像中的旋转框检测、小目标检测等核心问题；5. 未报告推理速度、计算量等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 课程学习（Curriculum Learning）策略可借鉴用于遥感目标检测模型的训练，从简单样本到困难样本逐步训练；2. 闭环RL优化思路可用于在轨模型的自适应更新；3. CoT推理链的思路可探索用于遥感场景下的目标推理；4. 但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Dynamic target tracking is essential for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in complex urban environments, where both the target and the camera viewpoint change continuously. Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies can track visible targets effectively, but their performance often degrades when buildings, vegetation, or roadside objects block the line of sight. During sustained occlusion, a policy may lose the target state, execute actions toward an incorrect region, and amplify this error through subsequent observations until re-acquisition becomes impossible. To this end, we present CosFly-VLA, a spatially aware VLA model that jointly grounds the target, estimates its visibility, and generates continuous flight actions through a structured prediction interface. To train this policy, we use a large-scale recipe over diverse data sources. Spatially Grounded Continued Pretraining (CPT) on a 500k mixed pool injects UAV-view depth, distance, and 3-D spatial reasoning. A three-stage Curriculum-based Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) process then specializes the tracker through multi-head warm-up followed by two-stage curriculum learning over natural and hard / long-occlusion data. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training subsequently teaches recovery-oriented reasoning traces before structured answers. Finally, a closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) stage optimizes tracking behavior with a multi-component reward covering stand-off tracking, grounding quality, collision avoidance, and task success. Relative to OpenVLA, CosFly-VLA-0.8B reduces open-loop Average Displacement Error (ADE) by 34.1% on seen-test and 35.3% on unseen-test. Closed-loop optimization improves Success Rate (SR) by 29.8% and 2.5%, respectively. These results demonstrate progress from visible-frame imitation toward spatially grounded action-closed-loop control, evaluated under a shared oracle state history.

</details>


### [132] [Risk-Aware Belief Control Barrier Functions over Random Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15016)
*Shaohang Han,Gang Chen,Yixi Cai,Ignacio Torroba,Ivan Stenius,Patric Jensfelt,Javier Alonso-Mora,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于风险感知信念控制障碍函数（BCBF）的框架，用于在未知动态环境中保障机器人安全，通过随机有限集（RFS）信念和SMC-PHD滤波器处理多目标状态不确定性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人安全控制（safe control）和信念控制障碍函数（BCBF），属于机器人控制/规划领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测模型或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，无需阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人安全控制（safe control）；场景：未知动态环境（unknown, dynamic environments），水下实验（underwater experiments）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：移动物体（moving objects），数量未知且时变。

Model architecture: 核心框架：风险感知信念控制障碍函数（risk-aware belief control barrier function, BCBF）；信念估计：随机有限集（RFS）信念，通过序贯蒙特卡洛概率假设密度滤波器（SMC-PHD filter）实现，用粒子集（set of particles）表示。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验方式：仿真（simulation）和真实水下实验（real-world underwater experiments）；具体数据集名称和评价指标：摘要未说明。

Experiments: 通过仿真和真实水下实验验证了所提方法的有效性和效率（effectiveness and efficiency），但未给出具体数值结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测模型或轻量化技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于机器人安全控制领域，不涉及遥感图像目标检测或模型轻量化，不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Ensuring robot safety in unknown, dynamic environments is a fundamental requirement. It involves inferring the states of an unknown and time-varying number of moving objects from noisy, incomplete measurements. We address safe control under the induced multi-object state uncertainty with a risk-aware belief control barrier function (BCBF) framework. The uncertainty is captured by a random finite set (RFS) belief, estimated by a sequential Monte Carlo probability hypothesis density (SMC-PHD) filter that represents it with a set of particles. Building directly on these particles, we construct a nonsmooth BCBF, establish forward invariance of the safe set under continuous prediction, and derive an explicit condition under which discrete updates preserve safety. Simulation and real-world underwater experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Agile Navigation in Crowded Environments for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15036)
*Shuyu Wu,Zeyu Liu,Tianbao Zhang,Fanxing Li,Fangyu Sun,Mingkang Xiong,Wei Xi,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VOP-Nav，将速度障碍法(VO)的几何安全性与端到端学习的敏捷适应性结合，用于四足机器人在动态拥挤环境中的导航，避免显式目标检测与跟踪。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦四足机器人导航与避障，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、星上部署等模型压缩与加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：四足机器人动态导航与避障；应用场景：室内外动态拥挤环境；传感器类型：LiDAR（多帧点云）；目标类别：行人/障碍物（非检测任务，无显式目标检测）。

Model architecture: VOP-Net：处理多帧LiDAR数据，隐式编码动态约束并预测基于速度障碍法(VO)的安全速度区域。网络输出作为导航策略的输入，同时VO预测在训练中作为奖励信号。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到在Unitree Go2四足机器人上进行了真实世界部署验证，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。部署平台为嵌入式机器人控制器，非星上/边缘计算场景。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真环境：Isaac Gym；真实环境：Unitree Go2四足机器人上的室内外动态环境。评价指标：成功率(success rate)、 locomotion speed、碰撞避免(collision avoidance)能力。

Experiments: 在Isaac Gym中与所有基线方法对比，VOP-Nav取得更高成功率，同时平衡运动速度与避碰性能。真实环境部署验证了鲁棒性和效率。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化或模型压缩；4. 方法针对机器人导航，无法直接迁移到遥感图像处理。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的启发点：VO理论中"隐式编码动态约束"的思路，在遥感在轨场景中可类比为"隐式编码时序上下文辅助小目标检测"，但关联性非常间接。

Abstract: Navigating dynamic and crowded environments presents significant challenges for quadruped robots due to severe sensor occlusion and unpredictable human motion. Existing approaches face a trade-off: model-based methods, such as Velocity Obstacles (VO), theoretically guarantee safety but rely on accurate obstacle motion estimates that often fail in dense crowds, while end-to-end learning methods offer robustness but lack motion prediction capability of obstacles, leading to collisions or conservative behaviors. To solve this, we propose VOP-Nav, a novel navigation system that combines the geometric safety of VO with the agile adaptability of end-to-end learning. Using only local onboard observations, our system avoids explicit obstacle detection and tracking pipelines. The VOP-Net processes multi-frame LiDAR data to implicitly encode dynamic constraints and predict a safe velocity region derived from Velocity Obstacle theory. Importantly, the VO predictions serve a dual role: they are used as input to the navigation policy during inference and as a reward signal during training to encourage safe motion. Evaluations in Isaac Gym demonstrate that VOP-Nav achieves higher success rates than all baselines while balancing locomotion speed and collision avoidance. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates the system's robustness and efficiency in complex indoor and outdoor dynamic environments.

</details>


### [134] [DriftWorld: Fast World Modeling through Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15065)
*Susie Lu,Haonan Chen,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DriftWorld，一种基于漂移生成模型的动作条件世界模型，通过单次前向传播替代扩散模型的多步迭代去噪，实现30+ fps的快速未来帧生成，用于机器人规划与策略评估。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人操控（robotic manipulation）领域的视觉世界模型与规划，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。虽然其"加速推理"的思路在广义上有借鉴意义，但场景、任务、数据模态均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：动作条件世界模型（action-conditioned world model）用于机器人规划与控制；应用场景：视觉机器人操控（vision-based robotic manipulation）；传感器类型：RGB相机；目标类别：机器人操控场景中的物体（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: DriftWorld基于漂移生成模型（drifting generative models），学习动作条件的漂移（action-conditioned drift），在训练阶段完成漂移学习，推理时从当前观测和候选动作序列通过单次前向传播生成未来帧，无需迭代去噪。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。其加速策略是架构层面的设计：用漂移模型替代扩散模型的多步采样，实现单步前向推理。推理速度30+ fps，比扩散基线平均快17倍。但这属于生成范式改变，而非模型压缩/轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及推理速度30+ fps（在机器人操控场景的硬件上），比扩散基线快17倍。未涉及星上/边缘部署相关讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, Robomimic（均为机器人操控基准数据集）；评价指标：决策性能（state-of-the-art decision-making performance）、推理时间（inference time）、rollout准确度、与真实策略的相关性（correlation with ground truth up to 0.99）。

Experiments: 在多个机器人操控基准上评估，DriftWorld在决策性能上达到SOTA，推理时间远少于扩散世界模型基线。作为离线模拟器时，rollout得分与真实策略相关性高达0.99。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，场景、任务、数据模态均不匹配；2. 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量、是否适合边缘/星上部署；3. 仅在机器人操控场景验证，未在遥感图像上测试；4. 未涉及目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但其"用单步生成替代多步迭代采样以加速推理"的思想有一定启发：在遥感在轨场景中，如果涉及生成式方法（如超分、去噪、数据增强等），可借鉴类似思路减少推理步骤。但该论文本身不涉及目标检测或轻量化压缩，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable robots to plan by imagining the outcomes of their actions, but their value for control hinges on generating many rollouts quickly. This creates a bottleneck for diffusion-based world models: multistep sampling makes each rollout expensive, limiting large-scale action search at inference time. We introduce DriftWorld, an action-conditioned world model based on drifting generative models. Rather than denoising iteratively at inference, DriftWorld learns an action-conditioned drift during training, allowing it to generate future frames from the current observation and a candidate action sequence in a single forward pass at 30+ fps, which is 17x faster on average than diffusion based baselines. We evaluate DriftWorld on standard vision-based robotic manipulation benchmarks, including Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, and Robomimic. By producing rollouts that are both accurate and fast, DriftWorld achieves state-of-the-art decision-making performance with far less inference time than diffusion-based world model baselines. Beyond online control, DriftWorld can also serve as an offline simulator for ranking real-world robot policies, with rollout-based scores correlating with ground truth at up to 0.99. These results show that drifting models are a strong fit for robot world modeling, where fast, high-quality imagination directly supports planning and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [135] [Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for Distributed ISAC-Enabled Vehicle Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15111)
*Wenjie Liu,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向无信号灯路口的分布式ISAC车辆协同框架，利用目标导向语义通信和MHPPO强化学习实现感知与通信一体化，降低信令开销并保证100%无碰撞。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于地面车辆路口协同场景，不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等轻量化技术。属于车联网/通信领域的感知-通信一体化研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：车辆状态感知与通信传输决策；应用场景：无信号灯路口车辆协同；传感器类型：RSU（路侧单元）感知信号；目标类别：车辆状态（位置、速度等）。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 核心框架：目标导向语义通信（GSC）框架；状态预测：扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）；决策优化：掩码混合近端策略优化（MHPPO）；传输设计：不确定性感知传输设计（UTD），含鲁棒波束赋形和基于VoI的时分功率分配。不涉及任何视觉检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。论文优化的是通信信令开销而非模型计算开销。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。论文讨论的是RSU与BS之间的通信效率，非星上/边缘计算部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：碰撞率（0%即100%无碰撞）、信令开销（显著降低）。

Experiments: 通过仿真验证，与预测性ISAC基线及多个消融基线对比，所提框架实现100%无碰撞且信令开销显著降低。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩内容。属于通信/控制领域，不涉及计算机视觉或深度学习模型轻量化。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，其"语义通信"中按信息价值（VoI）选择性传输的思想，在星地链路中或可类比为按场景重要性选择性回传检测结果，但与本课题核心（模型轻量化）无关。

Abstract: Vehicle coordination at unsignalized intersections relies on accurate real-time vehicle state acquisition and reliable command-and-control (C&C) signal delivery. However, existing studies typically treat sensing, communication, and control separately, which may lead to redundant transmissions, outdated state information, and unreliable vehicle coordination. In this paper, we investigate a new scenario of distributed integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled vehicle coordination at intersections, where multiple roadside units (RSUs) collaboratively transmit sensing signals for vehicle state acquisition and C&C signals for vehicle movement control under the management of a central base station (BS). To improve signaling efficiency, we propose a unified goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) framework, which transmits sensing and C&C signals only when they are semantically important for improving intersection traffic throughput. Specifically, an extended Kalman filter (EKF) is adopted to predict vehicle states and fuse distributed sensing measurements. A masked hybrid proximal policy optimization (MHPPO) framework is then developed to jointly determine sensing transmission decisions, C&C transmission decisions, and C&C signal contents based on a value-of-information (VoI) reward. Furthermore, we propose an uncertainty-aware transmission design (UTD), including robust beamforming and VoI-based time-division power allocation, to improve sensing and communication reliability under vehicle state uncertainty and inter-RSU interference. Simulation results show that our proposed framework achieves 100% collision-free vehicle coordination with significantly reduced signaling overhead compared with predictive ISAC baselines adapted from state-of-the-art related studies and several ablation baselines.

</details>


### [136] [Catch, Throw, Repeat: Planning for Human-Robot Partner Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15129)
*Jonathan Rainer Lippert,Kai Ploeger,Abir Chowdhury,Hermann Müller,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机协作抛接球（partner juggling）的实时规划与控制架构，实现机器人与人类在多球模式下的同步抛接。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是人机物理交互中的抛接球任务，属于机器人控制、运动规划与实时反馈领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人机协作抛接球（human-robot partner juggling）；应用场景：物理人机交互（physical HRI）；传感器类型：视觉追踪（predictive ball tracking）；目标类别：球（ball）。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 实时规划与控制架构，包含：predictive ball tracking（预测性球追踪）、adaptive online trajectory optimization using multiple-shooting formulation（基于多段打靶法的自适应在线轨迹优化）、state-machine-based coordination logic（基于状态机的协调逻辑）。不涉及任何深度学习目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然系统强调"real-time"，但这是指机器人控制层面的实时性，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 用户研究（user study）：8名参与者，技能从新手到专家不等。评价指标：连续接球次数（consecutive catches）、成功率（success rate）。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 所有参与者在10分钟测试中均超过此前报告的最佳结果；一名参与者将三球级联抛接记录提升5倍至20次连续接球；另一名参与者在单球接返任务中实现40次连续接球、100%成功率。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制与人机交互领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Dynamic object exchange between humans and robots remains a challenging problem due to uncertainty in perception, timing, and contact-rich interaction. Human-robot juggling represents a particularly demanding instance of this problem, requiring precise real-time coordination, predictive motion planning with feedback control, and robustness to variability in human motion. Enabling such skills is of interest for advancing physical human-robot interaction and shared autonomy. We present a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling that enables a robot to reliably catch and throw balls in synchronized multi-ball patterns with a human partner. The system integrates predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization using a multiple-shooting formulation, and a state-machine-based coordination logic to enable synchronized multi-ball human-robot partner juggling. In a user study with 8 participants of varying juggling skill from beginner to expert, we demonstrate that our system can achieve three-ball cascades shared between the robot and the human. All participants exceeded previously reported best-case results within a 10-minute test session, with one participant extending the previous record for shared three-ball cascade juggling fivefold to 20 consecutive robot catches, and another participant achieving a 100% success rate with 40 consecutive catches in a single-ball catch-and-return setting. Video documentation can be found at https://kai-ploeger.com/partner-juggling

</details>


### [137] [Assessing Physical Frailty and Fall-Risk Indicators with Social Robots: An in situ Evaluation with Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15156)
*Aniol Civit,Antonio Andriella,Alba Martínez,Joan Ars,Aida Ribera,Cristian Barrué,Guillem Alenyà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于社交机器人的老年人衰弱评估框架，利用行为树架构和视觉骨架追踪完成标准化临床测试，并与专业评估工具取得高度一致性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于医疗健康领域的老年人衰弱评估，使用社交机器人和视觉骨架追踪，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：老年人衰弱评估与跌倒风险评估；应用场景：康复中心/医疗保健；传感器类型：RGB摄像头（视觉骨架追踪）；目标类别：人体姿态/骨架关键点。完全不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: Behavior Tree架构，协调感知、决策、交互和测量模块；使用vision-based skeleton tracking。摘要未说明具体深度学习模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作部署在社交机器人上，而非星上/边缘设备。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：在康复中心研究实验室采集，N=81名老年人；评价指标：ICC（组内相关系数，>0.9）、Cohen's Kappa系数（k=0.67与治疗师对比，k=0.55与IMU对比）；临床测试：SPPB、TUG。

Experiments: 与治疗师评估、步态分析走道、惯性测量单元(IMU)进行对比验证；机器人测量的测试完成时间和步态参数与参考仪器高度一致(ICC>0.9)；SPPB总分与治疗师评估有substantial agreement(k=0.67)，与IMU有moderate agreement(k=0.55)。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化，与本研究方向无任何交集；2. 未讨论模型效率、实时推理性能；3. 仅在单一康复中心评估，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的点是：Behavior Tree架构用于多模块协调的思路，在星上多任务调度场景中或有参考价值，但关联度极低。

Abstract: Frailty assessments are crucial to evaluate the risk of adverse events and the health and social care needs of older adults, yet their administration remains resource-intensive and typically relies on coarse clinical outcomes, such as task completion times, which may overlook biomechanical indicators of functional decline. To address this, we present a robotic framework that guides older adults through standardised frailty and fall-risk tests while capturing clinical scores and additional frailty-related metrics, offering a deeper insight into a user's condition. The system uses a Behaviour Tree architecture that coordinates perception, decision-making, interaction, and measurement modules. Using vision-based skeleton tracking, the robot evaluates established clinical tests, including the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) and the Timed Up and Go (TUG). The framework was co-designed with healthcare professionals and evaluated in situ during six months in a rehabilitation centre's research lab with N=81 older adults. Robot-derived measurements were compared against therapist assessments and clinical reference instruments, including a gait analysis walkway and an inertial measurement unit (IMU). Results showed excellent agreement for most test completion times and gait-related parameters ($ICC > 0.9$). And, substantial agreement for the overall SPPB score comparing the robot and the therapist ($k = 0.67$) and moderate agreement comparing the robot and the IMU ($k=0.55$). The findings highlight that social robots can provide reliable and objective frailty assessments in healthcare settings while enabling the collection of relevant mobility indicators beyond conventional outcomes.

</details>


### [138] [Scaling Behavior Foundation Model for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15163)
*Weishuai Zeng,Kangning Yin,Xiaojie Niu,Shunlin Lu,Weixiang Zhong,Jiahe Chen,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Zirui Wang,Furui Xu,Ming Zhou,Kailin Li,Weinan Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人形机器人控制的Behavior Foundation Model (BFM)缩放配方，通过协调运动跟踪学习范式、策略数据与参考动作多样性、Humanoid Transformer架构，在仿真和真实部署中显著提升控制精度和泛化能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全面向人形机器人控制（humanoid control），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域无关；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。属于泛机器人/具身智能方向，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人形机器人全身协调控制（humanoid whole-body control）；应用场景：仿真环境与真实世界部署；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：人形机器人姿态/动作。

Model architecture: Humanoid Transformer，一种可扩展的模型架构，能够促进结构化行为表征的自然涌现。具体结构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是真实世界人形机器人部署，而非星上/边缘部署。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE)，在local mode和global mode下分别报告了超过10%和82%的误差降低。

Experiments: 在仿真和真实世界部署中进行了实验，相比已有的人形机器人控制器，在测试集上local mode下MPKPE降低超过10%，global mode下降低82%。消融实验和详细对比摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文本身未讨论遥感场景、目标检测任务或模型轻量化问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于人形机器人控制领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务场景、传感器模态、部署约束、评价指标等方面均无交集。Transformer架构设计思路（Humanoid Transformer）在结构上可能有极间接的参考价值，但缺乏轻量化相关讨论，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Humanoid control requires natural whole-body coordination, precise real-time responses to control signals, and robust generalization across diverse environmental contexts, making it a cornerstone for generalist embodied agents. Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have recently emerged as a promising solution to address these challenges by leveraging large-scale behavioral data to achieve superior expressiveness, versatility and generalization. However, despite growing interest in scaling BFMs to further improve their capabilities, it remains unclear how key factors, including the learning paradigm, behavioral data and model architecture should be coordinated to enable effective scaling. In this work, we revisit the scaling recipe for BFMs and demonstrate that substantial performance gains can be achieved through the coordination of three core components: 1) the learning paradigm of motion tracking that reformulates diverse humanoid control problems as the reproduction of integrated whole-body behaviors in the global frame; 2) the strategic synergy between on-policy rollout quantity and reference motion diversity; and 3) the expressive and scalable model architecture termed Humanoid Transformer that facilitates the natural emergence of structured behavioral representations. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world deployment, we demonstrate that our approach yields significant improvements in control fidelity and task generalization, reducing Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE) on the test set by over 10% in local mode and 82% in global mode compared with existing humanoid controllers. These results establish BFM as a principled and effective foundation for scalable and general-purpose humanoid control.

</details>


### [139] [AHEAD: Anticipatory Hand-Driven Teleoperation via Human Intent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15172)
*Seok Joon Kim,Junho Lee,Federica Spinola,Taein Kwon,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AHEAD，一种基于VR的遥操作机器人系统，通过注意力分类器预测操作者意图（抓取物体和放置槽位），减少机器人反应延迟并降低操作者负担。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是VR遥操作机器人系统（teleoperation），涉及手部运动预测、意图分类、人机交互，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：VR遥操作机器人抓取与放置（pick-and-place）；应用场景：人机交互、机器人遥操作；传感器类型：VR头显/手部追踪传感器；目标类别：抓取物体和放置槽位（grasp object, placement slot）。与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于注意力的分类器（attention-based classifier），输入为3D手部信号、头部信号和场景上下文，输出为意图预测（抓取物体和放置槽位）。结合状态机（state machine）将预测转换为稳定的机器人目标。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗、实时推理或边缘/星上部署。虽然涉及实时性（reaction latency），但这是指机器人执行延迟而非模型推理延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：用户实验数据（user study），非公开遥感数据集；评价指标：Top1准确率（grasp object 76%, target slot 76%）、机器人反应延迟（reaction latency，减少0.6s和1.4s）、操作者负担（operator load，通过用户报告评估）。

Experiments: 用户实验（user study）对比基线方法，AHEAD在意图预测Top1准确率上达到76%（抓取物体和放置槽位），机器人反应延迟分别减少0.6s（物体）和1.4s（槽位），操作者负担更低。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴的局限性信息。摘要未说明模型复杂度、泛化能力、在真实遥感场景下的表现等。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能间接相关的点是：论文使用注意力机制（attention-based classifier）处理时序信号进行意图预测，这种时序建模思路在遥感视频目标检测中可能有参考价值，但与本课题（遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化）的核心方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Direct hand-driven teleoperation maps an operator's hand motion to robot end-effector commands at every frame, enabling precise control, but it requires constant monitoring and correction during approach, grasp, and placement, which can be slow and fatiguing. For repetitive pick-and-place tasks, supervisory (goal-based) teleoperation simplifies this process: the operator specifies goals/waypoints, and the robot executes the motion using planning algorithms. Yet, this introduces latency, as the robot must wait for the next command before it can plan and act. "How can we reduce robot reaction time while lowering operator workload?" To tackle this question, we present AHEAD, a real-time VR teleoperation system that anticipates operator intent to enable proactive, hand-driven control. In a digital twin, the operator performs pick-and-place naturally, using hand motion to convey high-level commands rather than a continuous robot trajectory. AHEAD processes a short window of 3D hand and head signals together with scene context through an attention-based classifier to predict the intended grasp object and placement slot. A state machine converts intent predictions into stable robot goals, enabling early motion while remaining stable under noisy predictions and corrective hand movements. AHEAD's intent prediction module achieves Top1 accuracy: 76% for grasp objects and 76% for target slots. Moreover, our user study shows AHEAD reduces robot reaction latency by 0.6 s (object) and 1.4 s (slot) relative to baselines. Participants also reported lower operator load, indicating faster robot responses while maintaining low operator effort in practice.

</details>


### [140] [RoboTTT: Context Scaling for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15275)
*Yunfan Jiang,Yevgen Chebotar,Ruijie Zheng,Fengyuan Hu,Yunhao Ge,Jimmy Wu,Tianyuan Dai,Scott Reed,Li Fei-Fei,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboTTT，一种将机器人策略的视觉运动上下文扩展到8000时间步（比现有方法高三个数量级）的测试时训练方法，用于机器人操作任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人操作（robot manipulation）领域的视觉运动策略，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作策略（robot manipulation policies）；应用场景：真实机器人操作任务（real-robot manipulation tasks）；传感器类型：视觉传感器（visuomotor context）；目标类别：机器人抓取、组装等操作目标。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于Vision-Language-Action (VLA)策略的机器人基础模型，集成Test-Time Training (TTT)，序列模型的循环状态由快速权重（fast weights）构成，通过梯度下降在训练和推理时更新参数，将历史信息压缩到权重空间中。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然TTT在推理时也进行梯度更新，但这并非为模型压缩或加速设计，反而可能增加计算开销。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到"不增加推理延迟"（without growing inference latency），但这是相对于同等上下文长度的基线而言，并非针对边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实机器人操作任务（real-robot manipulation tasks），具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标：任务完成率、性能提升百分比（overall performance improvement by 87%）。

Experiments: 主要结果：相比单步上下文基线，整体性能提升87%；在5分钟10阶段组装任务上完全成功（baseline从未成功）；8K时间步上下文训练的模型比1K时间步的模型性能提升62%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 测试时训练（TTT）在推理时进行梯度更新，计算开销大，不适合资源受限的星上部署场景；4. 仅在机器人操作任务上验证，泛化到遥感场景的可能性极低。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能值得思考的点是：TTT将历史信息压缩到权重空间的思想，在概念上类似于在线适应（online adaptation），但星上计算资源有限，推理时做梯度更新不现实。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Recent robot foundation models operate with single-step or short-history visuomotor context. We introduce Test-Time-Training Robot Policies (RoboTTT), a robot model and training recipe that scale visuomotor context to 8K timesteps, three orders of magnitude beyond state-of-the-art policies, without growing inference latency. At this context length, we unlock new robot capabilities: one-shot in-context imitation from human video demonstrations, on-the-fly policy improvement, robustness to perturbations, and stronger performance on multi-stage, long-horizon tasks. We also observe, for the first time, steady gains in closed-loop performance as pretraining context length scales. At its core, RoboTTT integrates Test-Time Training into robot foundation models such as Vision-Language-Action policies, yielding a sequence model whose recurrent state consists of fast weights, parameters updated by gradient descent during both training and inference, compressing histories into weight space and retrieving contextual information for long-context conditioning. To scale training context length, the recipe combines sequence action forcing with truncated backpropagation through time. On challenging real-robot manipulation tasks, RoboTTT improves overall performance by 87% over the single-step context baseline and fully completes a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task, which no baseline ever does. RoboTTT trained with 8K-timestep context outperforms the same model pretrained with 1K timesteps by 62%, suggesting context length as a new scaling axis for robot foundation models. Videos are available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/

</details>

### [141] [PiVoT: A Variational Solution for Real-time Large-scale Multi-object Detection and Tracking under Heavy Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13891)
*Runze Gan,Qing Li,Simon J. Godsill,Mike E. Davies,James R. Hopgood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PiVoT，一种基于变分推理的无训练多目标检测与跟踪方法，面向噪声雷达点云，在汽车雷达数据集上实现实时性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向的是汽车雷达（automotive radar）点云的多目标检测与跟踪，属于地面自动驾驶感知领域，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及在轨部署或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多目标检测与跟踪（multi-object detection and tracking）；场景：汽车雷达（automotive radar）点云感知；传感器：雷达（radar），非遥感卫星传感器；目标类别：摘要未说明具体目标类别，泛指道路场景中的物体。

Model architecture: PiVoT基于贝叶斯框架和变分推理（variational inference），联合推断目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率，无需外部聚类或检测器。关键创新包括birth pruning、二次到线性复杂度缩减、高效Doppler Poisson模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文强调计算效率（quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions），但这是算法层面的复杂度优化，并非模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。且面向的是汽车平台而非卫星平台，不涉及星上部署约束。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：现代汽车雷达数据集（full-scale modern automotive radar datasets），非遥感数据集；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，仅提到性能与deep-learning detection benchmark相当。

Experiments: PiVoT在挑战性场景中显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器，可扩展到上千个目标，对视觉上不可区分的杂波具有鲁棒性，在完整汽车雷达数据集上实现实时运行，性能与深度学习检测基准相当。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星场景完全无关，不涉及遥感图像目标检测；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化技术；3. 无参数量、FLOPs等部署指标；4. 仅在汽车雷达数据集上验证，未在遥感数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可参考的点：其变分推理中的复杂度优化思路（quadratic-to-linear reduction）在算法效率设计上有一定启发性，但与本课题的"遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向关联度很低。

Abstract: Multi-object detection and tracking from noisy point clouds remain challenging in many data-scarce radar applications. Current Bayesian trackers based on Poisson measurement models offer a training-free solution but struggle to achieve accuracy and efficiency under severe clutter, large object populations, and full-resolution Doppler point clouds. We address this with PiVoT, a fast, clutter-resilient multi-object tracker for both positional and Doppler measurements. PiVoT performs end-to-end detection and tracking of a large and time-varying number of objects without external clustering or detectors, through joint inference of object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data association, and measurement rates. Its efficiency is driven by several variational inference innovations, such as theoretically justified birth pruning, quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions for exact updates, and a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experiments show that PiVoT substantially outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in challenging scenes, while also demonstrating exceptional scalability to a thousand objects, robustness to clutter visually inseparable from objects, and real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets, where it attains performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark as a training-free joint detector and tracker.

</details>


### [142] [A Temporal Machine Learning-Based Time-to-Event Model for Predicting ALS Progression and Healthcare Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14190)
*Zongliang Yue,Qi Li,Terry Heiman-Patterson,Frank Bearoff,Zhaohui Qin,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数字孪生启发的时序生存预测框架，用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者的功能衰退预测和辅助设备使用时间预测，属于生物医学/临床决策支持领域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全聚焦于ALS（肌萎缩侧索硬化症）的临床病程预测，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：疾病进展预测、生存分析、辅助设备使用时间预测；应用场景：临床决策支持、精准医学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 数字孪生启发的时序机器学习时间-事件（TTE）模型，结合Cox比例风险模型、广义加性混合模型（GAMM）、时序机器学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该模型面向临床环境，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自构建的ALS纵向数据集（包含诊断记录、ALSFRS-R评估、日常生活活动能力、人口统计学信息）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标（如C-index、AUC等未提及）。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果数值、对比方法或消融实验细节。仅提到Cox模型识别出下肢功能（行走、爬楼梯）是轮椅使用的最强预测因子。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于生物医学信息学/临床预测建模领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无任何技术交集。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive and heterogeneous neurodegenerative disease in which predicting clinically meaningful milestones, such as assistive device use, remains challenging. We developed a time-to-event, digital-twin-inspired framework that integrates longitudinal ALS Functional Rating Scale-Revised (ALSFRS-R) trajectories with survival modeling to support individualized prediction of functional decline and assistive device utilization. We constructed a harmonized longitudinal dataset by integrating diagnosis records, ALSFRS-R assessments, activities of daily living, and demographic information, followed by preprocessing to ensure data quality, temporal alignment, and cohort consistency. Correlation-based clustering identified coherent functional domains spanning bulbar, upper limb, axial, lower limb, and respiratory systems. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear, domain-specific functional decline across all domains. In addition, a temporal machine learning model was developed to predict longitudinal functional decline and capture stage-dependent disease progression. Cox proportional hazards modeling further identified lower limb function, particularly walking and stair climbing, as the strongest predictors of earlier wheelchair access. Building on these results, we implemented a digital twin-inspired temporal machine learning-based time-to-event (TTE) model that generates individualized survival curves and dynamically predicts wheelchair-free survival. This framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and clinically actionable approach for linking ALS progression with personalized decision support, with applications in proactive care planning, clinical trial stratification, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [143] [Supervised Fine-Tuning vs. In-Context Learning: An Equilibrium Analysis of LLM Personalization under Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14371)
*Fengzhuo Zhang,Zhuoran Yang,Dirk Bergemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型(LLM)服务中监督微调(SFT)与上下文学习(ICL)两种个性化方法的统计-经济权衡，分析用户选择、资源拥塞和平台策略问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于LLM服务的个性化算法选择(SFT vs ICL)及其经济/拥塞效应，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM个性化服务策略分析；应用场景：AI平台服务；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，实验使用GPT-2进行线性回归任务验证。

Lightweight method: 文中"轻量级"指In-Context Learning (ICL)相对于Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT)的计算开销更小，但并非模型压缩/加速领域的轻量化技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验使用GPT-2在linear regression tasks上验证理论预测；另外对21个主要AI平台的文档进行了调研。

Experiments: 理论分析为主，实验验证了ICL和SFT在不同条件下的性能排名预测；平台调研显示同时提供SFT和ICL的平台比例从2021年的9.5%增长到2025年的71.4%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、目标检测或轻量化技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于LLM服务经济学/运筹学方向，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化技术。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI services, but a critical tension emerges: while personalization improves model performance, it consumes scarce computational resources that users must share. When should a user invest in expensive Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) versus lightweight In-Context Learning (ICL)? How does congestion from other users' personalization choices reshape these incentives? And what strategies should platforms adopt when offering multiple personalization algorithms?
  We develop a tractable framework for LLM serving that captures the statistical-economic trade-offs users face. Our analysis yields several surprising insights. First, we show that ICL and SFT dominate in different regimes, determined by an interplay between pretraining coverage and data signal-to-noise ratios, but congestion can flip these rankings. Second, equilibrium resource consumption exhibits pronounced non-monotonicity: improving pretraining precision reduces the congestion, while broader pretraining coverage and harder tasks sometimes increase it. Third, we prove that offering both personalization methods never hurts the platform's maximal profits, despite potentially increasing computational load.
  Experiments with GPT-2 on linear regression tasks validate our theoretical predictions about algorithm performance. Complementing these results, our review of documentation from 21 major AI platforms shows that the share offering both SFT and ICL increased from 9.5% in 2021 to 71.4% in 2025, consistent with our platform-design implications.

</details>


### [144] [Position: Explainability Research Must Prioritize Foundations over Ad-hoc Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14123)
*Michal Moshkovitz,Suraj Srinivas,Lesia Semenova,Nave Frost,Cyrus Rashtchian,Valentyn Boreiko,Shichang Zhang,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Cynthia Rudin,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于可解释AI（XAI）的立场论文，批评现有XAI方法缺乏实际应用闭环，呼吁转向以人为中心、可行动的解释系统，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。它讨论的是可解释AI（XAI）领域的方法论缺陷和未来方向，属于泛机器学习/人机交互范畴，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型结构设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感或目标检测数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文仅提及对ICML、NeurIPS、ICLR论文的分析和XAI从业者调查，无目标检测或轻量化实验。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何技术借鉴。摘要本身作为立场论文，未提供具体技术方案或实验结果。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文讨论的是XAI领域的方法论问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of Explainable AI (XAI) techniques -- from feature attributions to sparse autoencoders -- explanations rarely influence real-world workflows. In practice, they are often generated and discarded without guiding meaningful action. This gap reflects foundational shortcomings: research has not yet established methodologies for integrating explanations into end-to-end, human-in-the-loop systems. This position paper argues that the machine learning community must pivot from ad-hoc XAI methods toward addressing foundational & structural challenges, including unclear problem formulations, underspecified evaluation objectives, and the absence of pipelines for explanation-driven feedback. We support this claim through an analysis of recent ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR papers and a survey of XAI practitioners, revealing recurring issues that limit cumulative progress. We conclude by outlining a practical checklist designed to shift XAI toward a more human-centered, action-oriented paradigm. By emphasizing foundational clarity over the development of ad-hoc methods, we hope to provide a roadmap for integrating explanations into actionable, feedback-driven AI systems.

</details>


### [145] [Adaptive Runge-Kutta Step Control Buys Training Loss, Not Generalization: An Honest Compute-Matched Study of RK-Adam Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14516)
*Akhilesh Gogikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将高阶Runge-Kutta积分器应用于Adam优化器（Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对），但在严格等计算量协议下，该RK变体在训练损失上不如普通Adam，且其"自适应性"是虚假的；修复后虽能大幅降低训练损失，但无法提升测试精度，最终结论是高阶自适应积分带来的收益可以被更廉价的一阶基线替代。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。本文研究的是深度学习优化器（Adam的RK积分变体），属于优化算法理论方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接或间接关系。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测模型、轻量化压缩或星上部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及任何具体视觉任务或应用场景，仅在通用神经网络训练框架下讨论优化器性能。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及具体模型架构，仅讨论优化器（Adam的RK变体）的设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。本文未提及具体数据集名称，评价指标为训练损失和测试准确率。

Experiments: 在严格等计算量（等梯度评估次数）协议下，RK变体在minibatch和full-batch训练中均不如plain Adam；修复后的变体在full-batch下训练损失比tuned Adam低约40倍，但测试精度未提升；n=10实验表明梯度平均是隐式正则化器，但RMSprop和NAdam以1/3的每步成本达到或超越其效果。

Limitations: 本文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无可借鉴的轻量化或部署信息。研究结论本身也表明高阶RK优化器在实际应用中缺乏优势。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。本文属于优化器理论研究，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测模型或轻量化技术。如果硬要说间接启发：优化器的选择对训练效果有显著影响，在遥感目标检测模型轻量化训练时，可关注Adam、AdamW、RMSprop等一阶优化器的调优，而非追求复杂的高阶方法。

Abstract: Interpreting optimizers as gradient-flow discretizations has motivated applying higher-order Runge-Kutta (RK) integrators to neural networks. We build a representative Adam variant (Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK pair, FSAL reuse, local-error step control) and evaluate it under a strict compute-matched protocol giving every method the same gradient-evaluation budget - an accounting this literature rarely enforces. Under it the RK variant loses to plain Adam on training loss in both minibatch and full-batch (RK's best-case) training. Instrumenting it shows the "adaptivity" is illusory: normalized error stays far below tolerance, the step size pins at its growth cap from step one (98-100 percent of steps), and no rtol x hmax x h0 setting makes it act; tolerances spanning 100x give bit-identical trajectories. The method is exactly fixed-step Adam with an averaged gradient at 3-4x cost. Repairing it (true reject branch; error on the applied map) reverses the full-batch result - about 40x lower training loss than tuned Adam - and a fixed-step control isolates adaptivity (an emergent warmup-and-growth schedule) as the mechanism. But the gain is fragile to the initial step size and does not reach test accuracy. A pre-registered follow-up rules out the obvious explanations: deeper minimization does not overfit, and an explicit temperature knob only hurts - leaving a trajectory effect, the controller selecting a minimum generalizing 1.3-3.4 points below first-order descent at equal depth. An n=10 study confirms one secondary effect: gradient averaging is a genuine implicit regularizer, beating lr-matched Adam and AdamW on 10/10 seeds - yet RMSprop and NAdam match or beat it at a third the per-step cost. Higher-order adaptive integration buys deeper deterministic minimization and a small regularization effect, but nothing a cheaper, well-tuned first-order baseline does not already provide.

</details>


### [146] [CARPRT: Class-Aware Zero-Shot Prompt Reweighting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14125)
*Ruijiang Dong,Zesheng Ye,Jianzhong Qi,Lei Feng,Feng Liu,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种类别感知的零样本提示重加权方法（CARPRT），通过为不同类别自适应分配提示权重来改进VLM零样本分类性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用视觉-语言模型的零样本分类提示重加权问题，虽然摘要中出现了"an aerial view of"作为提示示例，但方法本身是面向通用图像分类的，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：零样本图像分类（zero-shot image classification）；应用场景：通用图像分类；传感器类型：通用RGB图像；目标类别：通用类别（如cat, airport, apple等）。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 基于预训练VLM（vision-language model），通过计算图像与文本提示的相似度得分进行分类。核心改进是提出类别感知的提示重加权机制（CARPRT），为每个类别独立计算提示权重，而非使用类别间共享的固定权重。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。方法本身依赖预训练VLM（通常参数量较大），且需要为每个类别独立计算权重，推理开销可能高于传统方法，但摘要未给出具体效率数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：standard image classification benchmarks（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：分类准确率（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在标准图像分类基准上，CARPRT优于现有的类别无关重加权方法（class-independent reweighting methods），验证了建模提示-类别依赖关系对零样本预测的有效性。具体对比方法和数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，未在遥感数据集上验证；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；4. 依赖预训练VLM，模型规模大，推理效率未讨论；5. 需要为每个类别独立计算权重，当类别数很大时计算开销可能显著增加。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1. 如果未来在遥感场景中使用VLM进行零样本目标检测或场景分类，可借鉴其类别感知提示重加权的思路，针对遥感特殊类别（如不同舰船类型、飞机型号）自适应调整提示权重；2. 但该方法本身不涉及轻量化，且VLM模型通常较大，与在轨部署的轻量化需求相悖。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot image classification by computing the similarity score between an image and textual descriptions, typically formed by inserting a class label (e.g., "cat") into a prompt (e.g., "a photo of a"). Since the score for a given image-class pair is sensitive to the choice of prompt, existing studies ensemble multiple prompts using a weighting vector to aggregate scores across different prompts. Yet, in current strategies, the weighting vector assigned to each prompt is shared across all classes, implicitly assuming that prompts are conditionally independent of classes, which often does not hold in practice, as a prompt like "an aerial view of" might be apt for "airport" but ill-suited for "apple". To address this, we propose class-aware zero-shot prompt reweighting (CARPRT). This scoring scheme adjusts the weighting vector for each class label by capturing the class-specific relevance of different prompts in a training-free manner. For each class label and every available prompt, we quantify their class-specific relevance by averaging image-text relevance scores over images predicted to that class under the given prompt. These estimates are then normalized to derive class-specific weights. Evaluations on standard image classification benchmarks show that CARPRT outperforms existing class-independent reweighting methods, confirming that modeling prompt-class dependencies is crucial for effective zero-shot prediction and even broader VLM-based application settings that rely on prompt ensembling. Our code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CARPRT.

</details>


### [147] [Probabilistic Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Estimating Heterogeneous Elastic Properties from Low-Resolution and Noisy Displacement Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14563)
*Tatthapong Srikitrungruang,Jaesung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种概率逆弹性物理信息神经网络(PIE-PINN)，用于从低分辨率、含噪声的位移数据中鲁棒估计杨氏模量和泊松比。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是固体力学中的逆弹性问题，属于计算力学/物理信息神经网络(PINN)领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：逆弹性参数估计（杨氏模量、泊松比）；场景：固体力学/计算力学；传感器类型：位移测量（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: PIE-PINN框架：B样条引导的位移网络 + 神经网络校正 + 分层半柯西模型；使用拉普拉斯分布建模位移观测、应变差异和平衡残差。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向的是力学仿真/反问题求解，非边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；通过不同噪声水平和观测分辨率的案例研究进行验证；评价指标未明确列出。

Experiments: 在不同噪声水平和观测分辨率下进行了系统案例研究，展示了PIE-PINN的鲁棒性；与现有逆方法对比，但摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算效率或实时性；3. 仅针对弹性参数估计问题，无法迁移到视觉检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可能有间接启发：1) B样条引导网络的思想可用于遥感图像中的连续特征表示；2) 概率建模和自适应损失加权策略可借鉴到遥感目标检测中的噪声鲁棒性设计。但整体上不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Estimating spatially heterogeneous elastic properties from low-resolution displacement measurements is a severely ill-posed inverse elasticity problem because low resolution obscures spatial details needed to distinguish heterogeneous property variations, and small measurement perturbations or fitting errors are amplified through inverse estimation. Existing inverse methods often rely on high-fidelity observations and manually prespecified loss weights, limiting their adaptability and making them sensitive to noise and resolution degradation. We propose a Probabilistic Inverse Elasticity Physics-Informed Neural Network (PIE-PINN) framework for robust estimation of Young's modulus and Poisson's ratio from noisy, low-resolution displacement data. PIE-PINN models displacement observation, strain-discrepancy, and equilibrium residuals using Laplace distributions within a unified probabilistic model. To improve robustness, the framework combines a B-spline-guided displacement network with a hierarchical half-Cauchy model for displacement residual scales. The B-spline provides a smooth global representation of the displacement field, while the neural network correction captures local variations. The hierarchical scale model adaptively downweights severe displacement fitting errors, enabling more robust recovery of the latent mean displacement field. An alternating maximum-likelihood training strategy updates the mean through weighted residual minimization and updates the scales to adjust the loss weights. Systematic case studies across varying noise levels and observation resolutions demonstrate the robustness of PIE-PINN.

</details>


### [148] [Explainable Geospatial AI for Satellite Ground Station Siting Using LiDAR-Derived Terrain Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14127)
*Shohini Sarkar,Smithi Mahendran,Rishi Chudasama,Varun Mannam,Arav Luthra,Yuvraj Rekhi,Vivek Nadig,Arsh Goenka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LightGBM的机器学习框架，利用开放地理空间数据预测代表性杂波高度（RCH），用于无线电传播和干扰分析，相比ITU基线降低60%以上误差。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文虽然使用了光学遥感产品作为特征输入，但其核心任务是预测无线电传播中的杂波高度（RCH），属于射频规划/频谱协调领域，与遥感目标检测（object detection）和模型轻量化（lightweight）完全无关。不涉及任何目标检测任务，也不涉及模型压缩、加速或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：回归预测（RCH regression）；应用场景：无线电传播分析、低轨卫星地面站选址、频谱协调；传感器类型：LiDAR（用于生成标签）、光学遥感产品（作为特征）；目标类别：无目标检测，预测的是杂波高度标量值。

Model architecture: LightGBM（梯度提升决策树），非深度学习模型，非CNN/Transformer架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。模型本身是LightGBM，属于传统机器学习方法，参数量远小于深度神经网络，但论文未讨论模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。虽然LightGBM本身轻量，但论文未讨论星上或边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：USGS 3D Elevation Program（LiDAR标签）、全球土地覆盖/地形/人口/热/光学遥感产品（特征）；评价指标：MAE=1.79m, R²=0.765，还评估了米级误差、容忍带精度、过/低估尾部、与ITU杂波高度体制的一致性、SHAP可解释性。

Experiments: 对比了多个回归器，最终选择LightGBM；相比ITU基线绝对误差降低60%以上；进行了分割特征消融、非森林消融、土地覆盖匹配的国际验证。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感目标检测完全无关；2. 与模型轻量化完全无关；3. 未讨论在轨/星上部署；4. 使用传统机器学习而非深度学习，方法体系差异大。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能参考的是：论文使用了多源遥感特征（土地覆盖、光谱反射率等）进行预测，但这对目标检测轻量化课题帮助有限。不推荐作为参考论文。

Abstract: Representative clutter height (RCH) is a key parameter in radio propagation and interference analysis because it captures the dominant height of local obstructions that drive terminal clutter loss. Current practice often relies on fixed clutter heights assigned to land use classes in Recommendation ITU-R P.452-18, but this misses within class variation and can lead to conservative exclusion zones and poor site ranking for low Earth orbit ground station siting and spectrum coordination. We present an interpretable, globally deployable machine learning framework for predicting RCH from open geospatial data. The model is trained using LiDAR derived labels from the U.S. Geological Survey 3D Elevation Program and inference time features from global land-cover, terrain, demographic, thermal, and optical remote sensing products. We define RCH using a robust 75th percentile clutter height statistic, evaluate multiple regressors, and select LightGBM for its accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with feature attribution analysis. The final model achieves a mean absolute error of 1.79m and an R^2=0.765, reducing absolute error by more than 60% relative to the ITU baseline. Beyond aggregate fit, we evaluate domain facing criteria relevant to RF planning, including meter scale error, tolerance band accuracy, over and under estimation tails, agreement with ITU clutter height regimes, and SHAP-based physical plausibility. SHAP identifies tree canopy cover, land-cover semantics, and spectral reflectance as the most influential predictors. Studies on segmentation derived features, non-forest ablations, and land-cover matched international validation show that open geospatial data can improve clutter modeling at scale without sacrificing interpretability or deployability.

</details>


### [149] [Sharp Stability Threshold and Certification for Designing Stable Residual Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14576)
