*Hyemin Gu,Michael Tyrrell,Tuhin Sahai,Markos A. Katsoulakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出深度残差架构的"次线性增长原则"，通过控制残差块速度场的输入幅度指数 q ≤ 1 来保证训练稳定性，并给出了一种无参数修改方法将 Mamba 块的 q 从 5 降至 1。

Research relevance: 1 分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等模型压缩或加速方法。论文核心是深度残差架构训练稳定性的理论分析，属于纯深度学习理论方向，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题基本无关。

Reading priority: 1 分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务，也未指定应用场景或传感器类型。实验仅在 Mamba 和 PatchTST 上进行，属于时序/序列建模架构。

Model architecture: 深度残差架构（deep residual architectures），Mamba 块，PatchTST。核心概念是残差块的 velocity field（速度场）及其输入幅度指数 q。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS 等）。虽然提到了"parameter-free modification"，但这是为了调整架构的稳定性指数，而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何与星上/边缘部署相关的指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文仅在 Mamba 和 PatchTST 上验证了训练稳定性，未提及具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验在 Mamba 和 PatchTST 上验证了 q ≤ 1 变体的训练稳定性，结论是稳定性由输入幅度指数决定，而非归一化层的存在。未涉及目标检测或遥感任务的实验。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景，无法直接迁移；2. 不涉及模型轻量化或部署优化；3. 实验仅在序列模型上验证，未在 CNN 或 ViT 等视觉骨干上验证；4. 未讨论对检测精度的影响。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：如果在设计轻量化遥感目标检测网络时使用残差结构，可参考该理论确保训练稳定性，但该论文本身不提供任何轻量化或检测性能提升的方法。

Abstract: We propose \emph{the sublinear-growth principle} for deep residual architectures -- a sharp stability threshold on the input-magnitude exponent of every residual block's velocity field: $$\|v(x, t)\| \leq c\,\|x\|^q + b, \qquad q \in [0, 1].$$ The threshold $q = 1$ is established via two independent arguments. Classical ODE theory gives a global forward flow on $[0, T]$ at $q \le 1$ and exhibits divergent velocity fields at any $q > 1$. The optimal-control analysis, via the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, sharpens this to a selection statement: the training optimum is bang-bang on the boundary of the admissible class, so the optimum at $q > 1$ blows up while the optimum at $q \le 1$ is safe by construction. The exponent criterion $q \le 1$ is thereby a necessary and sufficient condition for stable training. It clarifies architectural placements that ensure the stability of training and inference, explaining, for instance, the stabilizing role of layer normalization. The sublinear-growth velocity fields form \emph{the right function space} on which forward dynamics, adjoint sensitivity, and architectural composition are all well-controlled. An arithmetic of input-magnitude exponents under the five operations that build residual blocks enables efficient certification of $q_k \le 1$ at the level of architectural primitives, in place of ad hoc trial and error in the search for stable neural architectural designs. A parameter-free modification reduces the supercritical Mamba block from $q = 5$ to $q = 1$ without layer normalization, demonstrating this point. Experiments on Mamba and PatchTST confirm that the $q \le 1$ variants train stably: the criterion is the input-magnitude exponent, not the presence of a normalization layer.

</details>


### [150] [Certified Domain Consistency for Multi-Domain Retrieval: Label-Free Per-Domain Contamination Control with Conformal Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14157)
*Jayakumar Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出C3R，一个即插即用的控制层，用于跨域检索时按域认证污染预算，在无法保证时选择弃权而非静默违规。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测、轻量化均无任何关联。论文研究的是跨域检索（cross-domain retrieval）中的域污染（domain contamination）控制问题，属于信息检索/NLP领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：跨域检索中的域污染控制；场景：混合多域语料库检索；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: C3R控制层，基于风险控制预测集（risk-controlling prediction sets）的两拆分方案（two-split scheme），与reranker无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"frozen-stack and reranker-agnostic"（堆栈冻结、与重排序器无关），但这并非星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：开放测试平台（包括联邦法规的独立公开数据集）；评价指标：污染预算（contamination budget）、召回率（recall）、认证污染（certified contamination）、弃权率（abstention rate）。

Experiments: 在千次重采样校准中，认证从未违规（稳定性结果），而边际控制在每次抽样中最污染的域均违规；软降级（soft demotion）在同等认证污染下比最强校准级联保留更多召回率；LLM判断的下游探测表明错误权威归因随污染上升、随控制下降。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何借鉴。摘要未说明方法在视觉任务上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于信息检索/可信AI领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化研究方向无交叉。

Abstract: Retrieval over corpora that mix several domains often returns relevant but wrong-domain evidence that ranking metrics miss and that conformal risk control bounds only marginally, under-covering the worst domains. This work introduces C3R, a drop-in control layer that, from an inferred domain posterior and no query-time label, certifies a per-domain contamination budget where feasible and otherwise abstains rather than silently violating; on the hardest domains it guarantees a reduction, not a tight bound. The core is a two-split scheme built on risk-controlling prediction sets, whose finite-sample transfer bound crosses from the inferred to the true domain with fully estimable slack, supports heterogeneous budgets, and inverts for deployment. Population validity rests on this bound and a controlled simulation; across a thousand resampled calibrations the certificate never violates (a stability result) while marginal control violates the most-contaminated domain in every draw, and soft demotion retains more recall than the strongest calibrated cascade at equal certified contamination. The method replicates across open testbeds including an independent one from public federal regulations, and an LLM-judged downstream probe indicates wrong-authority grounding rises with contamination and falls under control. The layer is frozen-stack and reranker-agnostic.

</details>


### [151] [What's in a Smoothness Constant? Tighter Rates for Local SGD with Bounded Second-order Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14731)
*Kumar Kshitij Patel,Rustem Islamov,Sebastian U Stich,Aurelien Lucchi,Eduard Gorbunov,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Local SGD在一般凸目标下基于二阶异质性假设的收敛界，并改进了下界，属于分布式优化理论工作。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文是纯分布式优化理论（Local SGD收敛性分析），不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩/加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感、卫星、目标检测任务或场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何神经网络模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何实验数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。仅有理论证明，无实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 纯理论工作，未涉及任何实际应用场景，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。Local SGD作为分布式优化方法，理论上可用于分布式卫星集群的模型训练或联邦学习场景，但本文仅讨论凸优化理论，未涉及深度学习模型或遥感数据，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Local SGD, also known as Federated Averaging, is a widely used distributed optimization algorithm. Although Local SGD often outperforms alternatives such as Mini-batch SGD in practice, theory still only partially explains when and why local updates help under realistic data heterogeneity. Recent work by [Patel et al., 2025] shows that a bounded second-order heterogeneity assumption captures the efficiency of Local SGD for strongly convex objectives, and conjectures that the same principle extends to the general convex setting. In this paper, we prove this conjecture by establishing an improved convergence guarantee for Local SGD on general convex objectives under bounded second-order heterogeneity. We also improve the best-known lower bounds for Local SGD in this setting, showing that our upper bounds are nearly tight. Together, these results provide a sharper, more fine-grained convergence theory for Local SGD. As a further application of our techniques, we provide a lower bound for serial SGD with replacement, showing how second-order heterogeneity captures the impact of rare high-curvature clients.

</details>


### [152] [QFireNet: A Quantum-Enhanced U-Net for Wildfire Segmentation from Sentinel-2 Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14160)
*Jaiman Munshi,Tanvi Tewary,Sawyer Bloom,Aidan Chu,Chetan Maviti,Kyon Winston-Bey,Harshit Badjatia,Farhan Kittur,Vardhan Madhavarapu,Varun Kota,Joshua Kwon,Nazia Rangwala-Vohra,Franz Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出量子-经典混合U-Net模型（QB-Net和QuFeX），在Sen2Fire卫星数据集上用于野火分割检测，量子变分电路嵌入U-Net瓶颈层，在F1分数上略优于经典U-Net基线。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。涉及卫星遥感（Sen2Fire、CaBuAr数据集）和图像分割，但任务为语义分割（非目标检测），且轻量化仅提及参数压缩（非核心贡献），量子方法对在轨部署的硬件可行性未讨论。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向相关性较弱。

Reading priority: 2/5 — 与课题方向弱相关。如研究方向涉及量子机器学习可阅读，否则优先级低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：语义分割（semantic image segmentation），非目标检测。场景：野火检测（wildfire detection）。传感器类型：卫星多光谱遥感（Sen2Fire数据集）。目标类别：火灾区域（二分类分割）。

Model architecture: 基础架构：U-Net。改进：在U-Net bottleneck处注入变分量子电路（variational quantum circuit），具体使用QuFeX和QB-Net两种量子ansatz。对比模型：经典FPN（Feature Pyramid Network）。

Lightweight method: 摘要中仅提及"compression of parameters"（参数压缩），但未说明具体方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）。量子方法本身可能带来计算开销，而非轻量化。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等标准轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。量子-经典混合模型在现有星上硬件（FPGA/NPU）上部署可行性极低，量子计算硬件尚未成熟到可在轨部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Sen2Fire（卫星遥感野火数据集）、CaBuAr（California Burned Areas，跨数据集验证）。评价指标：F1 score（主要指标）。

Experiments: QB-Net F1=31.18，QuFeX F1=30.79，经典U-Net基线F1=28.71，经典FPN F1=31.13。数据混合（data mixing）消除域偏移后，FPN F1提升至39.76。在CaBuAr上进行了跨数据集泛化验证。

Limitations: 1. 任务为语义分割而非目标检测，与课题方向不完全匹配。2. 量子方法在轨部署不现实，硬件成熟度低。3. 轻量化仅一笔带过，无具体方法细节和指标。4. F1分数整体偏低（<40），模型性能有限。5. 未报告推理速度、模型大小等工程实用性指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 量子方法目前不适合在轨部署，不建议直接借鉴。2. 数据混合（data mixing）消除地理域偏移的策略可迁移到遥感目标检测中，提升跨区域泛化性。3. FPN作为对比模型表现良好，可考虑在轻量目标检测器中引入FPN结构。4. 参数压缩的尝试方向值得关注，但本文未给出细节，需查阅原文。

Abstract: Wildfire detection from satellite imagery is a semantic image segmentation problem that has proven to be difficult due to challenges such as class imbalance, feature complexity, and atmospheric interference. In this paper, we build on the foundational U-Net image segmentation model to develop a quantum-hybrid solution in hopes of more effectively modeling the high-dimensional spectral feature space of the Sen2Fire dataset. We inject a variational quantum circuit in the bottleneck portion of U-Net, specifically the QuFeX and QB-Net ansatzes. We test a classical Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) for further comparative analysis of the model, and we also explore classical improvements to the U-Net model and its training process, including a compression of parameters, alternative loss functions, and uniform mixing of input data. Our primary finding is that under matched conditions, both QB-Net (with an $F_1$ score of 31.18) and QuFeX ($F_1 = 30.79$) outperformed the classical U-Net baseline results ($F_1 = 28.71$). Additionally, the classical FPN achieved a comparable score of 31.13. A crucial finding was that data mixing removed a significant domain shift between the geographically-separated train and test sets, which boosted the classical FPN $F_1$ score to 39.76. We validate the architecture's robustness and generalizability to the wildfire detection problem via cross-dataset transfer on the California Burned Areas (CaBuAr) dataset. Overall, we find that quantum machine learning has potential to provide an advantage in the problem of wildfire image segmentation, and further experiments will continue to validate and expand upon this finding.

</details>


### [153] [GAttNHP: Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process for Extrapolation Reasoning in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14733)
*Xiangni Tian,Kaixian Yu,Runpeng Dai,Niansheng Tang,Hongtu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于分组注意力神经霍克斯过程的时序知识图谱预测框架，解决长程依赖、事件链交互和重尾时间分布三个难题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是时序知识图谱（TKG）上的事件预测，属于知识图谱与时序建模领域，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。没有任何遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能相关的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：时序知识图谱上的实体预测与时间预测；场景：知识图谱推理/事件预测；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：知识图谱中的实体与关系。

Model architecture: GAttNHP框架包含三个组件：(1) 自注意力编码器，将主体-关系链建模为连续时间点过程；(2) 语义软分组模块，通过可学习的霍克斯先验生成交叉注意力掩码；(3) Non-Crossing Quantile (NCQ) 回归头，提供校准的分位数估计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 六个TKG基准数据集；评价指标：实体预测（entity prediction）和时间预测（time prediction）指标。

Experiments: 在六个TKG基准数据集上，GAttNHP在实体预测和时间预测上均优于SOTA基线；消融实验确认最大增益来自长尾事件链。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型计算开销、推理速度等对部署关键的信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可能有间接启发：(1) 自注意力机制处理长程依赖的思路可用于遥感视频序列中的时序目标检测；(2) 软分组/跨注意力掩码思想可类比于遥感多类别目标检测中的类别感知特征交互；(3) NCQ回归处理重尾分布的思想，可用于遥感小目标检测中边界框回归的分布建模。但这些均为间接类比，不直接适用于遥感在轨场景。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs) record how facts evolve over time, but forecasting future events on a TKG remains difficult for three reasons: (i) long-range temporal dependencies are hard to encode; (ii) events on different chains mutually excite or inhibit one another in ways that snapshot-level models cannot express; and (iii) inter-arrival times are heavy-tailed and statistically sparse, so deterministic time predictors are unreliable. We address these three issues with a single framework, the \textbf{Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process (GAttNHP)}, built around three matched components. First, a self-attention encoder casts each subject--relation chain as a continuous-time point process and captures the lingering excitation of distant history. Second, a semantic soft-grouping module turns globally learnable Hawkes priors into an analytical cross-attention mask, so chains share excitation patterns through their latent group memberships rather than through exhaustive pairwise computation. Third, a Non-Crossing Quantile (NCQ) regression head replaces mean-based time prediction, providing calibrated, monotonically ordered quantile estimates that remain stable under heavy-tailed inter-arrival distributions. On six benchmark TKG datasets, GAttNHP improves over state-of-the-art baselines on both entity prediction and time prediction, and ablations confirm that its largest gains arise on the long-tail event chains where existing models fail most severely.

</details>


### [154] [Branching Policy Optimization: Sandbox-Native Language Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14171)
*Bowei He,Yankai Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向可执行沙箱环境的强化学习算法BPO，利用沙箱的确定性、可快照和可恢复特性，通过分支轨迹拓扑结构降低方差，提升LLM智能体在交互任务中的训练效率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM智能体的强化学习训练算法（BPO），属于自然语言处理/强化学习领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM智能体在可执行沙箱环境中的强化学习训练。应用场景：WebShop（网页购物）、ALFWorld（家居任务）、SWE-bench Verified（软件工程）。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基座模型为Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B，属于大语言模型架构。BPO算法本身不修改模型架构，而是改变强化学习的轨迹采样拓扑结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。7B-8B规模的LLM本身不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：WebShop、ALFWorld、SWE-bench Verified。评价指标：任务成功率（success rate）、梯度范数方差（gradient-norm variance）、策略更新次数（policy updates）。

Experiments: 在三个基准上，BPO相比GRPO和RLOO提升3.6-6.1个绝对百分点的成功率；梯度范数方差减半；在同等性能下减少38%的策略更新次数。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 方法依赖沙箱环境的确定性、可快照和可恢复特性，不适用于遥感图像处理等非交互式环境；3. 7B-8B模型规模过大，无法部署在星上边缘设备。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可抽象思考：BPO中"分支轨迹共享前缀以降低方差"的思想，在遥感模型轻量化场景中是否有类比？例如在NAS搜索轻量网络架构时，共享搜索路径前缀来加速搜索过程——但这属于非常间接的启发。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as the dominant paradigm for training large language model (LLM) agents that interact with executable sandboxes. State-of-the-art algorithms such as PPO, RLOO, and GRPO inherit their rollout topology from RLHF: for each prompt, N independent trajectories are sampled from the initial state, and an advantage is computed by subtracting a group baseline. This design ignores a defining property of agent sandboxes. They are deterministic, snapshottable, and resumable from any intermediate state. We argue that this property enables a fundamentally different rollout topology: rather than N independent trees of depth T, one can construct a single tree of N leaves whose siblings share prefixes, and therefore share variance. We instantiate this idea as Branching Policy Optimization (BPO), a sandbox-native RL algorithm that (i) adaptively snapshots the sandbox at high-entropy decision points along a backbone trajectory, (ii) forks K alternative actions per branch point and rolls out each to termination, and (iii) computes per-step advantages from sibling returns rather than from independent prompts. We prove this estimator is unbiased and has strictly lower variance than the trajectory-level baseline, with the reduction equal to the prefix-explained portion of return variance. On WebShop, ALFWorld, and SWE-bench Verified with Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B backbones, BPO improves success by 3.6--6.1 absolute points over GRPO and RLOO at matched compute, halves gradient-norm variance, and matches the best baseline using 38% fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [155] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究的是设备维护中的块替换策略优化问题，将块替换策略下的最优替换间隔学习建模为随机多臂老虎机问题，提出置信下界算法和Kaplan-Meier非参数估计方法，并分析了两种平均成本MDP。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是工业设备维护（block replacement policy）中的最优替换间隔学习问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然使用了机器学习方法（多臂老虎机、MDP），但应用场景、技术路线和评价指标均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：设备维护策略优化（块替换策略）；应用场景：工业设备运维；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何与遥感或目标检测相关的内容。

Model architecture: 不适用。本文不涉及深度学习模型架构，而是使用多臂老虎机（MAB）算法框架，包括Hoeffding-based LCB、Bernstein-based LCB、相关变体算法以及Kaplan-Meier非参数估计方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。本文不涉及星上或边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用合成数据（数值实验），未使用任何遥感或视觉数据集；评价指标：累积遗憾（regret）、策略一致性（policy consistency）、成本差距（cost gap）。

Experiments: 通过数值实验验证了理论结果，比较了不同算法的遗憾界，展示了最优块替换策略与年龄依赖替换策略之间的结构性成本差距。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩内容。本文的MAB框架和MDP分析虽然理论上严谨，但无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务中。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。本文研究的是设备维护策略优化，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [156] [How Much of a 10-K Matters? Aggregation-Dependent Value of Full-Text versus Risk-Factor Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14174)
*Sanggyu Sean Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究金融情感提取，利用10-K文件的风险披露部分，通过监督词典学习方法训练情感分数以预测回报和波动率，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于金融自然语言处理（NLP）领域，研究10-K文件的情感提取与市场波动预测，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能、目标检测、轻量化等方向完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：金融情感提取（sentiment extraction）；场景：10-K文件风险披露文本分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何与遥感或目标检测相关的内容。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及"supervised lexicon-learning approach"（监督词典学习方法）和"Loughran-McDonald dictionary baseline"。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1,383份10-K文件，来自94家Nasdaq-100科技公司（2006-2023年）。评价指标：分类准确率（classification accuracy）、与市场结果的相关系数（correlation with realised market outcomes）、定性词汇内容分析（qualitative lexical content）。

Experiments: 实验比较了三种聚合层级（sector/portfolio/individual firm）和两种标签（return/volatility）下的情感指标表现。全文本在sector和portfolio层级表现更好，但Item 1A部分在individual-firm层级表现更优。Loughran-McDonald词典基线在所有层级与价格呈强负相关。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何相关局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于金融NLP领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉或可迁移的技术方法。

Abstract: Financial sentiment extraction has largely relied on news text and supervised extraction against return labels alone, leaving 10-K filings -- and volatility, the target risk disclosure is arguably best suited to informing -- comparatively unexplored. We extend a supervised lexicon-learning approach to 10-K filings and their Item 1A risk-factor sections, training sentiment scores against both return and volatility labels at three levels of aggregation: sector, portfolio, and individual firm. Across 1,383 filings from 94 Nasdaq-100 technology constituents (2006--2023), we evaluate the resulting twelve sentiment metrics on classification accuracy, correlation with realised market outcomes, and qualitative lexical content. Full-filing text produces more accurate sentiment at the sector and portfolio level for both targets, but this reverses at the individual-firm level, where the narrower Item 1A section performs better -- an effect we attribute to the interaction between document volume and the amount of independent training signal available at each level of aggregation. A Loughran-McDonald dictionary baseline is consistently, strongly negatively correlated with price at every level tested, underscoring the value of a supervised approach for regulatory disclosure text. These findings, and the design choices they motivate, establish the sentiment-generation methodology underlying a subsequent, larger-scale, multi-source system.

</details>


### [157] [Low-Latency Relay Selection in NR-V2X Vehicular Communications via Graph Isomorphism Networks with Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14176)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini,Pierpaolo Salvo,Paola Vocca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图神经网络（GINE）的边缘感知学习优化框架，用于C-V2X网络中实时中继选择，并引入GINE剪枝MILP的混合策略（GP-MILP）以平衡最优性与实时性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于C-V2X（蜂窝车联网）中的中继选择与通信优化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉任务；不涉及轻量化模型、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。虽然文中使用了"pruning"一词，但指的是对MILP搜索空间的剪枝，而非神经网络剪枝。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：C-V2X网络中实时中继选择（relay selection）；场景：密集城市环境下的车联网通信；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: GINE（Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features）图神经网络，用于边级别的中继激活预测。输入为有向图：节点特征编码车辆状态和流量需求，边特征编码无线链路容量。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明神经网络层面的轻量化方法。文中"pruning"指GINE预测结果用于剪枝MILP搜索空间（GP-MILP），属于优化求解加速，而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 推理延迟：所有测试实例在5ms内完成；GP-MILP求解器运行时间：超过98%的图实例在30ms以下。但这是通信网络场景的延迟指标，与星上部署无关。参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗均未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：通过OSM-SUMO-GEMV²管道生成的大规模数据集；评价指标：链路级准确率（accuracy 0.9589）、F1-score（0.9544）、端到端连接增益（相比1跳MILP基线提升最高9.2%和12%）。

Experiments: GINE在链路级决策上接近MILP最优解（准确率0.9589，F1 0.9544）；GP-MILP在保持MILP等效解（相同目标值）的同时，将求解时间压缩至30ms以下（>98%实例）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量等轻量化关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可间接借鉴：(1) 将优化问题建模为图结构并用GNN求解的思路，可用于遥感图像中目标间关系建模或场景图理解；(2) "Learning-to-Optimize"范式——用神经网络逼近组合优化问题，可类比于用轻量网络逼近传统检测算法的思路；(3) 混合策略（神经网络预测+精确求解器剪枝）的思想，可类比于轻量模型粗检测+大模型精修的两阶段策略。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency uplink connectivity is a key requirement for C-V2X networks in dense urban environments, where fast channel variations and blockages often degrade direct vehicle-to-infrastructure links. Multi-hop relaying can restore coverage, but relay-link activation under radio, capacity, and routing constraints results in an NP-hard optimisation problem, typically solved via Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), whose runtime scales poorly with graph size. This paper introduces an edge-aware Learning-to-Optimise framework for real-time relay selection. Each V2X snapshot is modelled as a directed graph: node features encode vehicle state and traffic demand, while edge features capture radio-link capacity. An offline MILP oracle generates optimal relay configurations that supervise a Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE), enabling edge-level relay activation through a single forward pass, with tightly bounded inference latency. To bridge learning and exact optimisation, we also propose a hybrid GINE-Pruned MILP (GP-MILP) strategy in which GINE predictions prune the MILP search space. Experiments on a large-scale dataset generated via an OSM-SUMO-GEMV$^2$ pipeline show that GINE closely matches MILP decisions at the link level (accuracy 0.9589), F1-score (0.9544) on validation) and yields consistent end-to-end connectivity gains over a 1-hop MILP baseline (up to 9.2% with four RSUs and 12% with two RSUs). Inference latency remains tightly bounded, with all evaluated instances completing within 5~ms. Moreover, GP-MILP preserves MILP-equivalent solutions (same objective value) while achieving solver runtimes below 30~ms for more than 98%) of the graph instances, making MILP-grade optimisation compatible with stringent NR-V2X latency budgets.

</details>


### [158] [RENEW: Towards Learning World Models and Repairing Model Exploitation from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14180)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Mykel Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用人类偏好反馈（DLHF）来修复离线强化学习中世界模型的利用问题，并引入RENEW方法提升样本效率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于离线强化学习（offline RL）领域，研究世界模型（world model）的利用问题（model exploitation），不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：离线强化学习中的世界模型动力学学习；应用场景：Jumanji和经典控制环境；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 世界模型（world model），基于Bradley-Terry偏好损失进行动力学模型微调；RENEW方法利用认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）聚焦微调。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: Jumanji环境和经典控制环境（classic control environments）；评价指标：样本效率（sample efficiency）、灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）、利用程度（exploitation）。

Experiments: 在Jumanji和经典控制环境上评估，naive DLHF需要大量偏好预算，RENEW提升了样本效率、限制了灾难性遗忘、减少了预训练世界模型中的利用问题。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于离线强化学习领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化，与课题方向无交集。

Abstract: World models are widely used in offline reinforcement learning (RL) to improve sample efficiency and generate experience beyond a fixed dataset. However, they are vulnerable to model exploitation where data coverage is thin. Prior work addresses this either by collecting more expert demonstrations, which is often expensive, unsafe, or unavailable, or by conservative algorithms that avoid uncertain regions, which limits generalization. We propose instead to repair exploitation directly using human preferences over imagined rollouts, leveraging the strong intuitive physics that allows humans to easily spot egregious dynamics hallucinations. We formalize this as Dynamics Learning from Human Feedback (DLHF), a Bradley-Terry preference loss over trajectory log-likelihoods under a learned dynamics model. Unfortunately, naive DLHF is sample inefficient, so we introduce RENEW, which uses epistemic uncertainty to focus finetuning where the model is most exploitable. We evaluate on several Jumanji and classic control environments and find that while naive DLHF requires an outsize preference budget, RENEW makes the framework practical by improving sample efficiency, limiting catastrophic forgetting, and reducing exploitation in pretrained world models. Taken together, our results provide initial evidence that preferences can supervise world model dynamics directly, offering a new approach to addressing exploitation in offline model-based RL.

</details>


### [159] [Closed-Loop Knowledge Dynamics: An Operational Framework for Saturation and Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14185)
*Xuening Wu,Shan Yu,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个三层次操作框架，用Lyapunov漂移条件和核函数差异来形式化分析闭环知识系统（如LLM、强化学习）中的反馈饱和与结构干预逃逸问题，属于机器学习理论工作。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化，而是研究大语言模型、强化学习和贝叶斯优化中的闭环反馈系统饱和与结构干预理论。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星相关任务。应用场景为LLM代码修复、稀疏奖励强化学习、贝叶斯优化，属于通用机器学习理论。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出三层次操作框架，涉及状态演化转移核K_θ、结构参数θ、观测等价类、吸引子与盆地等理论概念。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何部署相关信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。案例研究涉及LLM代码修复、稀疏奖励强化学习、贝叶斯优化，但未给出定量实验设置。

Experiments: 摘要未给出定量实验结果。仅提及案例研究使用matched continuation controls说明反馈强度和alignment对逃逸质量的影响。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文是纯理论工作，缺乏工程落地和遥感场景验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，几乎无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：论文讨论的"反馈饱和"概念在模型持续学习/增量学习场景中可能有类比意义（如在轨模型持续更新时性能饱和问题），但非常间接且缺乏具体方法映射。

Abstract: Feedback-driven loops support iterative improvement in large language models, reinforcement learning, and autonomous discovery, yet their gains often diminish under repeated internal feedback. We study why closed-loop knowledge systems saturate and what external information can move them beyond their current attractors. We introduce a three-level operational framework in which knowledge states $x_t$ evolve through transition kernels $K_θ$ indexed by a structural parameter $θ$. The governing structure is defined as the observational equivalence class of $θ$ induced by these kernels, while attractors and basins are properties of the fixed-$θ$ dynamics. A structural intervention changes $θ$ and produces a detectable kernel discrepancy on pre-specified probe states, making structural change falsifiable. Using a Lyapunov drift condition, we show that stable internal dynamics approach bounded stability regions with exponentially attenuated transients and a noise-controlled residual floor. We characterize escape through a metric condition on intervention-induced attractor displacement and a baseline-relative KL lower bound for increasing escape probability. This analysis also explains why conditional mutual information alone cannot certify escape: it measures variation among intervention-conditioned updates rather than departure from the no-intervention law. Case studies in LLM code repair, sparse-reward reinforcement learning, and Bayesian optimization use matched continuation controls to illustrate how feedback strength and alignment affect quality-improving escape. Our contribution is an operational connection among stability tools, measurable intervention effects, and cross-domain diagnostics.

</details>


### [160] [TEDDY: A Pediatric Foundation Model for Risk Forewarning from ICD-Coded Diagnostic Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14191)
*Matthew Brady Neeley,Jorge Botas,Johnathan Jia,Lin Yao,Daniel Palacios,Benjamin Choi,Zhandong Liu,Hyun-Hwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TEDDY，一个用于儿科电子健康记录诊断轨迹建模的轻量级解码器Transformer（184万参数），在疾病发病预测任务上优于更大规模的通用模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于儿科电子健康记录（EHR）的疾病发病预测，属于医学/临床信息学领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关概念，也不涉及目标检测或遥感场景理解。虽然模型本身是轻量级（184万参数）且使用了Transformer架构，但应用场景和任务类型与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"无任何直接或间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：疾病发病预测（disease-onset prediction）、就诊时间预测（visit-timing prediction）；应用场景：儿科电子健康记录；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：ICD-10诊断编码（16个章节，797种疾病）。

Model architecture: TEDDY：1.84M参数的decoder-only Transformer，在约7300万条ICD-10诊断记录上训练，建模纵向诊断轨迹和就诊时间。

Lightweight method: 模型本身是轻量级设计（1.84M参数），但摘要未说明具体的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量之外的部署相关信息（FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等）。模型仅1.84M参数，理论上适合边缘部署，但应用场景是医疗而非遥感卫星。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：单中心儿科机构约160万儿童的7300万条ICD-10诊断记录；评价指标：AUC（中位72.0%）、mean absolute restricted mean survival-time error（3.0天/365天）；对比基线：DenseNet、CNN、RNN、LSTM及更大规模通用语言模型。

Experiments: 在797个疾病发病预测任务上，TEDDY中位AUC 72.0%，在96-99%任务上优于DenseNet(50.0%)、CNN(57.2%)、RNN(60.1%)、LSTM(62.7%)；在哮喘(79.3%)和ADHD(84.7%)任务上优于大三个数量级的通用语言模型(62.7%/71.7%)；在罕见病预测上表现突出（90%罕见病AUC置信区间高于随机水平）；预测信号在首次诊断前2年以上可检测。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：1）与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2）就诊时间预测的中位数和长尾返回间隔校准不佳；3）单中心数据，泛化性未知；4）未涉及任何遥感图像处理或目标检测技术。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点是：1）用极小的模型（1.84M参数）在特定领域任务上超越大模型，说明领域专用轻量模型的设计思路值得借鉴；2）decoder-only Transformer架构在序列建模上的有效性。但这些与遥感在轨目标检测的视觉任务性质差异巨大，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Pediatric electronic health records capture developmentally structured clinical trajectories, yet their potential for generative healthcare foundation models remains largely unexplored. Here we present TEDDY (Temporal Event Decoder for Disease in Youth), a 1.84-million-parameter decoder transformer trained on approximately 73 million ICD-10 diagnoses from 1.6 million children at a single pediatric institution. TEDDY models longitudinal diagnosis trajectories and visit timing. Predictions were made before visit codes were revealed, limited to first occurrences, and evaluated against sex- and age-matched controls. Across 797 disease-onset prediction tasks spanning 16 ICD-10 chapters, TEDDY achieved a median AUC of 72.0%, outperforming same-data DenseNet (50.0%), CNN (57.2%), RNN (60.1%), and LSTM (62.7%) baselines on 96-99% of tasks. Performance held across sex and age and was strongest among lower-prevalence diagnoses; 202 of the 225 rarest conditions (90%) had 95% confidence intervals above chance. Predictive signal remained detectable more than two years before first recorded diagnosis, with median AUCs of 59.7% in the unrestricted analysis and 64.4% in a fixed-cohort sensitivity analysis. In asthma and attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder benchmarks, AUCs were 79.3% and 84.7%, compared with 62.7% and 71.7% for the strongest comparators, including a general-purpose language model three orders of magnitude larger. Visit-timing predictions had a 3.0-day mean absolute restricted mean survival-time error over 365 days, although median and long-tail return intervals remained miscalibrated. Together, these results establish pediatric diagnostic histories as a substrate for compact generative models supporting broad, rare-disease, and long-horizon risk forecasting without population-scale data or billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [161] [Long-term User Engagement Optimization through Model-agnostic Downstream Rewards Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14192)
*Dingsu Wang,Filip Ryzner,Kelly He,Armando Ordorica,David Woo,Aditya Mantha,Liyao Lu,Usha Amrutha Nookala,Haoran Guo,Jiacong He,Olafur Gudmundsson,Matt Chun,Krystal Benitez,Dhruvil Deven Badani,Yijie Dylan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向推荐系统的、模型无关的下游奖励框架，用于优化长期用户留存，不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是推荐系统中的长期用户留存优化问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。属于泛机器学习/推荐系统方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：推荐系统长期用户价值优化；应用场景：Pinterest平台的Homefeed、Related Pins、Search、Notifications；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及是"model-agnostic"（模型无关）的下游奖励框架，可应用于多种排序模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是推荐系统在Pinterest平台上的生产部署，与星上/边缘部署无关。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Pinterest平台内部数据（Homefeed, Related Pins, Search, Notifications）；评价指标：engagement和retention-related metrics（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在线A/B实验表明在用户参与度和留存相关指标上有一致性改进，框架已在多个Pinterest页面部署。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究推荐系统长期用户留存优化，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: As recommender systems mature in the past few years, their optimization objectives have evolved from a primary focusing on short-term behavioral signals to a broader emphasis on long-term user engagement and retention. However, directly optimizing retention is difficult because return signals are sparse, delayed, and only partially attributable to earlier recommendations. Prior work has addressed this challenge with sequential modeling and reinforcement learning, but these approaches typically require task specific reward engineering, substantial computational overhead, and surface specific implementations that are difficult to generalize. In this paper, we present a unified, model-agnostic downstream reward framework for optimizing long-term user value in large-scale recommendation systems. First, we formulate the downstream reward learning problem and develop an offline screening framework to identify session level behaviors that are both observable early and predictive of future retention. We then propose several model-agnostic downstream rewards signals derived from observed user action patterns across multiple sources. We further discuss the engineering effort to productionize the proposed rewards derivations and challenges we faced when adding them to our ranking models. Online A/B experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in engagement and retention-related metrics, and the framework has been deployed across multiple Pinterest surfaces, including Homefeed, Related Pins, Search, and Notifications.

</details>


### [162] [Augmentations for Robust and Efficient Imitation Learning in Streamed Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14200)
*Somjit Nath,Abdelhak Lemkhenter,Pallavi Choudhury,Chris Lovett,Katja Hofmann,Sergio Valcarcel Macua,Lukas Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向流媒体游戏场景的时空数据增强方法，模拟低带宽网络下的像素块、模糊、鬼影等伪影，提升模仿学习策略的鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于3D视频游戏中的模仿学习与流媒体伪影鲁棒性，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：模仿学习（imitation learning）用于游戏智能体控制；场景：3D视频游戏流媒体环境；传感器类型：游戏画面（RGB视觉输入）；目标类别：游戏动作策略映射，非目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 基于预测逆动力学模型（Predictive Inverse Dynamics Models, PIDM），结合未来状态条件化与逆动力学策略在隐空间中的学习。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个现代3D视频游戏任务（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：评估性能（evaluation performance）、性能下降百分比（degradation percentage）。

Experiments: 在稳定流媒体条件下，使用时空增强训练的智能体相比未增强的智能体在相同数据预算下性能提升高达41%；引入网络延迟后，增强训练的智能体性能仅下降7.45%，而原始数据训练的智能体下降49.82%。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对游戏流媒体场景，与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型压缩或轻量化技术；3. 未讨论遥感图像中常见的传感器噪声、光照变化、几何畸变等退化因素。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可借鉴其"数据增强模拟部署环境退化"的思路：在遥感在轨场景中，可考虑模拟星上传输链路的带宽限制、压缩伪影、辐射校正误差等退化因素，设计针对性的数据增强策略来提升在轨检测模型的鲁棒性。不过该论文本身不提供遥感或轻量化方面的直接参考。

Abstract: Imitation learning is an appealing way to scale game-playing agents to complex 3D environments by training policies to map visual observations to actions from human demonstrations. However, these demonstrations are expensive to collect and modern game-playing is often done through streaming in which network delay and compression introduce spatiotemporally correlated visual artifacts that can cause a covariance shift at test time. To address these challenges, we propose streaming augmentations that mimic four types of artifacts commonly encountered during streaming with low-bandwidth network connection: pixelated blocks and scrubs, global blur, and ghosting. We instantiate our approach on top of predictive inverse dynamics models (PIDM), which combine future-state conditioning with an inverse dynamics policy in a learned latent space, and evaluate the impact of our augmentations across three tasks in modern 3D video games. Under stable streaming conditions, agents trained with spatiotemporal augmentations achieve up to 41% higher evaluation performance compared to agents trained without augmentations under an identical data budget. When network lag is introduced, agents trained with augmentations degrade by only 7.45% vs 49.82% of the original performance for agents trained only with the original data. These results clearly indicate that spatiotemporal augmentations tailored for the streaming setting are a simple yet powerful tool to train robust and efficient game-playing agents.

</details>


### [163] [Privacy Leakage in Federated Learning in Radiology Reports: A Comparative Evaluation of Tokenizer-Driven Privacy Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14205)
*Santhosh Parampottupadam,Andres Martinez,Dimitrios Bounias,Sinem Sav,Klaus Maier-Hein,Ralf Floca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究联邦学习中放射学报告文本的梯度反演攻击，比较不同分词器对隐私泄露的影响，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究联邦学习场景下放射学报告文本的梯度反演隐私泄露问题，属于医学自然语言处理(NLP)与隐私安全方向。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：联邦学习梯度反演攻击下的文本重建。场景：医学放射学报告隐私保护。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: GPT-2-style transformer，序列长度32。使用三种分词器：GPT-2 tokenizer、RadBERT tokenizer、LLaMA-2 tokenizer。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：公共放射学语料库（368,751份诊断报告、98,206份出院小结、1,500份MIMIC-CXR自由文本报告）。评价指标：精确句子重建率(Exact sentence reconstruction)、S-BLEU、临床术语恢复率。批次大小：64、128、256。

Experiments: 6个FL客户端训练GPT-2风格transformer。精确句子重建率在31%-44%之间（27个tokenizer×dataset×batch-size组合中为30.6%-43.5%）。批次64时出院数据集准确率：GPT-2 42.1%、RadBERT 42.3%、LLaMA-2 39.4%；批次256时降至37.3%、37.2%、34.3%。RadBERT恢复临床术语最多（18.1%）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的模型结构、轻量化方法或部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于医学NLP隐私安全领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务、场景、方法、指标上均无交集。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multi-institutional training on clinical text without sharing raw data, but gradient inversion can reconstruct sensitive information from shared model updates. The extent of this leakage for radiology reports, and the role of tokenizer design, remains unclear. We quantify gradient-based text reconstruction in FL and compare privacy risk across three tokenizers with the model architecture held fixed. Six FL clients trained a GPT-2-style transformer (sequence length 32) on public radiology corpora (368,751 diagnostic reports, 98,206 discharge summaries, 1,500 MIMIC-CXR free-text reports) using the GPT-2, RadBERT, and LLaMA-2 tokenizers at batch sizes of 64, 128, and 256. Assuming an active malicious server that modifies the shared architecture before distribution, we applied analytic gradient inversion and measured reconstruction fidelity over five runs. Exact sentence reconstruction ranged from 31% to 44% across tokenizers (30.6-43.5% across the 27 tokenizer x dataset x batch-size cells). At batch size 64 on the Discharge dataset, accuracy was 42.1% (GPT-2), 42.3% (RadBERT), and 39.4% (LLaMA-2), decreasing to 37.3%, 37.2%, and 34.3% at batch size 256. S-BLEU declined as batch size grew (GPT-2: 0.44 to 0.33; RadBERT: 0.48 to 0.35). RadBERT yielded the highest reconstruction fidelity and recovered the most clinical terms (18.1% of a 1,440-term reference vocabulary, vs 12.5% for GPT-2 and 9.4% for LLaMA-2), yet no tokenizer prevented leakage. Substantial portions of report text are therefore recoverable from FL gradients even at larger batch sizes and with domain-specific tokenizers. Tokenizer design influences leakage severity and is a privacy-relevant decision, not only a utility one; safeguards such as secure aggregation and differential privacy are likely necessary to meet HIPAA and GDPR requirements for FL in radiology NLP.

</details>


### [164] [LIGO-PINN: Learned Initialization via Gated Optimization to Alleviate Convergence Failures in Physics Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14233)
*Nilay Anurag,Shital Adhikari,Taniya Kapoor,Nikhil Muralidhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LIGO-PINN框架，通过门控逐层优化学习初始化权重，解决物理信息神经网络在偏微分方程建模中的收敛失败问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在偏微分方程（PDE）建模中的初始化策略优化，属于科学计算/物理机器学习领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩/部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：PDE求解/物理建模；应用场景：1D/2D/3D PDE域（含流体动力学）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: LIGO-PINN框架，基于门控逐层优化（Gated Layerwise Optimization）学习PINN的初始化权重。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1D和2D PDE域（含2D流体动力学设置），以及3D非结构化域验证；评价指标：平均性能提升百分比（91.5% over six baselines, 81% over strongest baseline）。

Experiments: 在1D/2D PDE域上评估，与六种基线方法对比，平均性能提升91.5%；在最强基线上提升81%；验证了在3D非结构化域上的泛化能力；分析了训练动力学。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于科学计算领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have had a broad research impact in modeling domains governed by partial differential equations (PDE). However, PINNs have been shown to perform poorly, sometimes even converging to trivial solutions, in challenging PDE domains, or when generalizing to unseen but related PDE domains. Previously proposed solutions detail hyperparameter tuning to reduce loss imbalance between data-driven and physics guided losses, curriculum learning based training strategies, or dynamic re-sampling of hard collocation points. These methods face certain pitfalls: hyperparameter tuning is expensive, designing a training curriculum is ambiguous in multi-parameter PDE settings, and dynamic resampling still fails in complex PDE settings. Complementary to this line of thinking, we believe the initial PINN network weights also play a crucial role in the emergence of catastrophic failures during training, yet the effect of PINN weight initialization has been surprisingly under-investigated. To this end, we propose a framework for Learned Initialization via Gated Layerwise Optimization (LIGO-PINN) to overcome PINN convergence failures. Through rigorous evaluation on 1D and 2D PDE domains, including a challenging 2D fluid dynamics setting, we demonstrate that our methodology outperforms state-of-the-art methods designed to alleviate PINN failures, achieving a 91.5% average performance improvement across six baselines and 81% over the strongest baseline. We also verify that LIGO-PINN generalizes to 3D unstructured domains. Finally, we analyze training dynamics across all three PDE domains to explain both LIGO-PINN's improvement and the convergence failure of traditional PINNs. Code: https://github.com/scailab/ligo-pinn
  Keywords: Machine Learning, Physics-Informed Neural Networks, Deep Learning, PDE Modeling

</details>


### [165] [MIDiff: Tackling Sparsity and Imbalance in Mobile Usage Generation via Multivariate-Imaging Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14249)
*Yilai Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散模型的多元移动使用轨迹生成框架MIDiff，通过C-GASF将稀疏多元序列转换为相关图像，并用U-Net+Triple Attention生成逼真的移动使用数据。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是移动设备使用轨迹（mobile usage traces）的生成，属于用户行为建模/时间序列生成领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多元时间序列生成（multivariate time series generation）；场景：移动设备使用行为建模；传感器类型：无（移动应用使用日志）；目标类别：无。摘要未说明任何遥感相关任务或场景。

Model architecture: 基于扩散模型（diffusion-based framework），使用C-GASF（Cross-Gramian Angular Sum Field）将稀疏多元序列转换为图像表示，U-Net架构中引入Triple Attention以保持时间一致性和变量依赖关系。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：移动使用轨迹数据（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：Discriminative Accuracy (DA)，对比基线为ZITS-VAE（DA=0.3476），MIDiff达到DA=0.1526（越低越好）。

Experiments: MIDiff在保真度指标上达到SOTA，Discriminative Accuracy优于最强基线ZITS-VAE。摘要未提供消融实验、参数量对比、推理速度等详细信息。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。该论文属于时间序列生成领域，应用场景、技术路线、评价指标均不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接参考的是C-GASF将稀疏序列转换为图像表示的思想，但遥感图像本身已是密集图像，无需此类转换。扩散模型在遥感目标检测中虽有应用，但本文聚焦于数据生成而非检测，且未涉及轻量化。

Abstract: Mobile usage traces are critical for tasks such as user behavior prediction and app recommendation, yet their use is constrained by privacy restrictions and costly large-scale data collection. Although generative models perform well on general time series, their application to mobile usage data remains challenging because (i) limited user activity causes severe sparsity, (ii) heterogeneous variable types complicate joint modeling, and (iii) functional differences across apps create pronounced usage imbalance. To address these challenges, we propose Multivariate-Imaging Diffusion (MIDiff), a diffusion-based framework operating in an imaging space defined by Cross-Gramian Angular Sum Field (C-GASF). C-GASF transforms sparse multivariate sequences into correlation images, while MIDiff employs Triple Attention in a U-Net to preserve temporal consistency and variable dependencies. Experiments show that MIDiff achieves state-of-the-art performance across fidelity metrics. In particular, it obtains a Discriminative Accuracy (DA) of 0.1526, compared with 0.3476 for the strongest baseline, ZITS-VAE, demonstrating its effectiveness in generating realistic and diverse mobile usage traces. Our code is available at https://github.com/YilaiLiu-HKU/MIDiff.

</details>


### [166] [Local Additive Feature Attribution: A Mathematical Taxonomy and Reporting Checklist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14271)
*Rebecca Afriyie Sarpong,Daniel Commey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于局部加性特征归因方法的综述，系统梳理了Shapley值、路径积分、梯度/反向传播、扰动和CAM类方法的数学假设与规范选择，并提出报告清单。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是XAI（可解释人工智能）领域的特征归因方法综述，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然可解释性方法可用于分析检测模型，但摘要中没有任何遥感、目标检测或轻量化的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非后续研究涉及轻量化模型的可解释性分析，否则无需关注。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文是XAI方法综述，不涉及具体任务场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是特征归因方法的数学框架（Shapley值、路径积分、梯度/反向传播、扰动、CAM），而非任何具体的检测或轻量化模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何实验数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。该论文是综述性质，未报告实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文是XAI综述，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。摘要未讨论任何与遥感、目标检测或模型压缩相关的内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然该论文本身不直接贡献于遥感在轨目标检测轻量化，但其提出的特征归因方法（如Shapley值、梯度归因）可间接用于分析轻量化模型（如剪枝后模型）的决策行为，帮助理解哪些特征对检测结果贡献最大，从而指导更合理的剪枝或蒸馏策略。但摘要本身未提供任何可直接借鉴的技术细节。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods are central to explainable artificial intelligence. Their assumptions are expressed in several mathematical languages: cooperative-game values, path integrals, gradient operators, perturbation distributions, and backpropagation rules. This survey proposes a common framework for local additive feature attribution. It organizes Shapley, path-based, gradient/backpropagation, perturbation, and CAM-style methods around five specification choices: value function, reference, path, perturbation distribution, and conservation rule. It then compares these methods through an axiom-by-method matrix and links common failure modes, including baseline sensitivity, off-manifold perturbations, sanity-check failures, adversarial manipulation, and method disagreement, to the assumptions that produce them. Finally, the survey proposes a ten-item reporting checklist for studies that use local additive attributions. The central message is that attribution results are meaningful only relative to the mathematical assumptions under which they are defined, and that those assumptions should be reported.

</details>


### [167] [Lyapunov Guidance: A Unified Framework for Stabilizing Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14272)
*Jingdong Zhang,Xinze Li,Yize Jiang,Luan Yang,Minkai Xu,Junhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LyaGuide，一种基于Lyapunov控制的流匹配引导框架，为引导流匹配提供显式稳定性保证，适用于图像逆问题、强化学习规划等任务。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接或间接关系。论文核心是生成式模型（flow matching）的引导方法，属于生成建模/控制理论交叉方向，不涉及遥感图像理解、目标检测或模型压缩部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：流匹配（flow matching）的引导（guidance）方法；应用场景：图像逆问题、强化学习规划、基于能量的建模；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Lyapunov控制的引导框架，提出伪投影算子（pseudo-projection operator）以施加Lyapunov条件，支持模型驱动和数据驱动两种设置。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文提到"minimal additional computational overhead"（最小额外计算开销），但这是指引导方法的附加开销，并非模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文仅提及"computational efficiency"（计算效率），但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在合成基准、图像逆问题、强化学习规划、基于能量的建模上进行，具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标涉及样本质量（sample quality）、引导保真度（guidance fidelity）、鲁棒性（robustness）。

Experiments: 在合成基准、图像逆问题、强化学习规划、基于能量的建模上进行了广泛实验，结果表明在样本质量、引导保真度和鲁棒性方面有一致改进，同时保持计算效率。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或边缘部署技术；4. 实验场景不包含遥感/卫星图像。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。Lyapunov控制理论在生成模型中的应用属于不同领域，不适用于遥感在轨目标检测模型的轻量化问题。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as an effective framework for learning complex data distributions, but adapting pretrained flow models to new tasks often requires computationally expensive retraining. Post-training guidance provides a more efficient alternative, but existing methods are largely heuristic and offer no explicit stability guarantees. We address this limitation by proposing LyaGuide, a unified Lyapunov-guided framework that formulates flow guidance as a Lyapunov control problem. Our main theoretical result establishes an equivalence between guided flow matching and Lyapunov control, thereby unifying common guidance strategies, such as classifier guidance, reward guidance, and energy-based guidance, within a single control-theoretic framework. To enforce the Lyapunov condition, we introduce a pseudo-projection operator with a closed-form expression that endows learned or heuristic guidance terms with explicit stability guarantees. LyaGuide supports two practical settings: a model-driven setting, where the target guidance distribution is specified through a known Lyapunov function, and a data-driven setting, where the guidance is adapted from task-specific downstream data. LyaGuide is compatible with existing guidance methods, introduces minimal additional computational overhead, and is straightforward to integrate in practice. Extensive experiments on synthetic benchmarks, image inverse problems, reinforcement learning planning, and energy-based modeling demonstrate consistent improvements in sample quality, guidance fidelity, and robustness, while maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [168] [NeuroGRIP: Retrieval-Augmented Graph Refinement for Knowledge-Grounded EEG Seizure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14314)
*Lincan Li,Zheng Chen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeuroGRIP框架，利用检索增强的图精炼方法，结合外部医学知识库校准EEG脑电图中的噪声图结构，用于癫痫发作检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是EEG脑电信号癫痫诊断，属于生物医学信号处理领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：癫痫发作检测（seizure detection）；场景：临床脑电图诊断；传感器：EEG电极；目标类别：癫痫发作/非发作。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 基于STGNN（时空图神经网络）的EEG脑网络表示学习，结合检索增强的图精炼框架NeuroGRIP，使用FAISS进行语义相似度搜索，大语言模型构建知识图谱。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向临床诊断而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: TUSZ和CHB-MIT两个EEG癫痫数据集；评价指标为seizure detection accuracy。

Experiments: 在TUSZ和CHB-MIT上验证，NeuroGRIP提升了癫痫检测准确率并增强了可解释性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或遥感相关方法。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于生物医学信号处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心研究方向。

Abstract: Seizure diagnosis from EEG signals is a critical yet persistently challenging task, due to the complicated neural dynamics and the spurious connections in inter-channel modeling. While spatial-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) have advanced EEG brain network representation learning, the resulting graph structures suffer from low clinical plausibility and limited interpretability due to their purely data-driven nature. To this end, we introduce NeuroGRIP, a retrieval-augmented graph refinement framework that incorporates external medical knowledge to calibrate noisy EEG graphs. We first construct a large-scale, domain-specific knowledge base derived from authoritative clinical guidelines. Leveraging large language models, we extract structured biomedical entities and relations to form a textual knowledge graph (KG), which serves as external knowledge source of clinical priors. Our framework performs alignment-aware query construction by projecting STGNN-generated EEG node embeddings into the semantic space of KG. Semantic queries are then executed via FAISS-based similarity search over knowledge triplets to retrieve relation evidence. Each predicted edge is assigned a confidence score based on retrieved similarity, relation type, and source reliability, enabling us to prune medically implausible edges from the originally predicted graph. Extensive experiments on TUSZ and CHB-MIT demonstrate that NeuroGRIP not only improves seizure detection accuracy but also enhances interpretability by grounding each prediction in clinically validated knowledge. This work provides the first unified framework that tightly couples brain dynamics with external medical expertise via retrieval-augmented reasoning, paving the way for knowledge-enhanced, explainable clinical diagnosis. The code is available at: https://github.com/LincanLi-X/NeuroGRIP.

</details>


### [169] [Towards a Unified Multidimensional Explainability Metric: Evaluating Trustworthiness in AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14315)
*Georgios Makridis,Georgios Fatouros,Athanasios Kiourtis,Dimitrios Kotios,Vasileios Koukos,Dimosthenis Kyriazis,Jonh Soldatos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个评估XAI方法（LIME、SHAP等）可解释性的统一框架，构建多维可解释性评分，并建立离线知识库用于上下文相关的可解释性评估。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，而是聚焦于机器学习模型的可解释性评估（XAI），属于AI可解释性方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何传感器类型。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及使用了多种机器学习模型和XAI方法（LIME、SHAP）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化/模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了三个开源数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）。评估指标为可解释性的三个维度：fidelity（保真度）、simplicity（简洁性）、stability（稳定性）。

Experiments: 在三个开源数据集上进行了基准实验，构建了离线知识库，分析了数据集特征对可解释性评分的影响。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果硬要说，其"多维评分+知识库"的评估框架思路可类比用于构建轻量化模型的综合评估体系（如同时评估精度、参数量、延迟、能耗等），但方向差异过大，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a comprehensive framework for assessing the explainability of various XAI methods, such as LIME and SHAP, across multiple datasets and machine learning models, with the ultimate goal of creating a unified multidimensional explainability score. Our methodology focuses on three key aspects of explainability: fidelity, simplicity, and stability. We leverage benchmarking experiments to systematically evaluate these aspects and use the insights gained to construct an offline knowledge base. This knowledge base captures the explainability scores for each registered model and serves as a valuable resource for context-dependent evaluation of explainability. By analyzing the complementary characteristics and metadata of AI models, datasets, and XAI methods, the knowledge base will enable the estimation of explainability scores for previously unseen datasets and models. Properties like fidelity, simplicity, and stability may vary significantly based on the dataset, underlying model, and domain expertise of the end user. We demonstrate our framework by applying it to three open-source datasets, discussing the implications of the obtained results in relation to the characteristics of the datasets. Our work contributes to the growing field of XAI by providing a robust and versatile tool for evaluating and comparing the explainability of various XAI methods, ultimately supporting the development of more transparent and trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [170] [Counterfactual Optimal Action Trees (COAT): Interpretable Prescriptive Policies from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14318)
*Youssef Drissi,Markus Ettl,Shivaram Subramanian,Wei Sun,Zack Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COAT框架，结合反事实推断与混合整数优化，从观测数据中学习可解释的规范性策略，应用于航空辅助定价并取得显著收益提升。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于因果推断、运筹优化和商业定价决策，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：反事实规范性策略学习（prescriptive policy learning）；应用场景：航空辅助定价（airline ancillary pricing）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: COAT框架：反事实结果估计（counterfactual outcome estimation）+ 大规模混合整数优化（large-scale mixed-integer optimization）+ 列生成（column generation）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何边缘/星上部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：与一家全球主要航空公司合作的17周现场试点（field pilot）数据；评价指标：每次预订的附加收入提升（upsell revenue per booking），提升6.9%。

Experiments: 17周现场试点实验，COAT使每次预订的附加收入提升6.9%，航空公司预计在美国国内合格市场每年增加5000万至1.5亿美元的增量高级座位收入。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于因果推断与运筹优化在商业定价中的应用，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: We introduce COAT (Counterfactual Optimal Action Tree), a framework for learning interpretable prescriptive policies from observational data. COAT combines counterfactual outcome estimation with large-scale mixed-integer optimization, using column generation to translate causal predictions into feasible, transparent decisions under business and regulatory constraints. We apply COAT to airline ancillary pricing, a setting characterized by complex business rules and limited experimental flexibility. In a 17-week field pilot with a major global airline, COAT increased upsell revenue per booking by 6.9%, with the airline projecting \$50-\$150 million in incremental annual premium seat revenue across eligible domestic markets. The success of the pilot led to scaled adoption and informed broader AI-driven decision initiatives within the organization.

</details>


### [171] [Value Leakage: An LLM's Answers Are Silently Shaped by Its Own Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14345)
*Jan Betley,Johannes Treutlein,Jan Dubiński,Harry Mayne,Karol Gałązka,Niels Warncke,Anna Sztyber-Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型在回答用户问题时隐藏自身价值观偏见的"隐性价值泄露"问题，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于大语言模型对齐与安全性研究，讨论的是模型价值观泄露（covert value leakage）问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化等方向完全无关。没有任何遥感场景、目标检测任务或轻量化部署相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。该论文属于AI安全与对齐领域，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化的任何内容。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为评估语言模型在问答中的价值偏见，场景为通用AI对话，不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关任务或传感器类型。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。仅提及Claude Opus 4.8、Qwen等模型名称，未涉及任何目标检测模型或轻量化网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用自建评估套件（a suite of evaluations）来量化价值泄露，但未提及任何遥感领域标准数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）或目标检测指标（mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。仅提及在Fermi-estimation任务上Claude和Qwen模型表现不同，未涉及目标检测对比实验或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何相关局限性分析。摘要本身未讨论遥感或目标检测领域的任何问题。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是大语言模型的对齐与安全性问题，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在任务目标、技术方法、应用场景上均无交集，无法提供可借鉴的技术思路或可复现方向。

Abstract: People use language models for practical questions whose answers are difficult to verify. We show that models exhibit covert value leakage: the information they provide is influenced by their own values, without this influence being disclosed to the user.
  In one of our evaluations, the user is considering investing in an AI company and wants to know how likely the AI bubble is to pop. Claude Opus 4.8 gives a lower probability when the company under consideration is Anthropic rather than OpenAI. Yet Claude mostly fails to disclose this influence to the user.
  Covert value leakage is a form of misalignment because it goes against the user's preferences and is likely to mislead them. To investigate this phenomenon, we introduce a suite of evaluations to quantify value leakage and whether models disclose it. We find that models are influenced by different types of values, including preferences for morally good outcomes, for the company that developed them, and for some human leisure activities over others.
  We often observe large differences among frontier models on the same evaluation. For example, on a Fermi-estimation task, Claude models falsely claim to give unbiased answers in their chain-of-thought, while Qwen models explain how their values bias their answers. Value leakage is a failure mode distinct from sycophancy and reward hacking, and current alignment training and evaluations do not adequately address it.

</details>


### [172] [Learning Who to Treat When Treatment is Missing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14346)
*Johnna Sundberg,Rayid Ghani,Eli Ben-Michael,Edward Kennedy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算约束下策略学习中的缺失治疗数据处理问题，将ATE估计中的高效估计方法扩展到策略价值和CATE估计，提出基于MAR和MCCAR假设的估计器。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是因果推断/策略学习（policy learning）中的缺失数据处理，属于统计学习和决策理论范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及遥感或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及神经网络模型结构设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。仅提及使用synthetic和semi-synthetic数据集进行实验。

Experiments: 实验使用合成和半合成数据集验证了缺失机制正确设定的重要性：错误设定的估计器无论样本量大小都存在偏差，而所提估计器在假设满足时达到近oracle性能。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于因果推断和策略学习领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉点。

Abstract: Policy learning methods are increasingly used to inform treatment allocation under budget constraints. Most proposed methods assume complete treatment data, yet applications frequently suffer from missingness that can bias estimates and lead to suboptimal policies. We address this gap by extending efficient estimators for average treatment effect (ATE) estimation to policy value and conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimation under missing at random (MAR) and missing completely conditionally at random (MCCAR) treatment data. Through asymptotic efficiency analysis, we prove that the MAR estimator, which leverages partially-observed units, is both valid and more efficient than the MCCAR estimator when MCCAR assumptions hold. This result provides formal justification for preferring MAR-based estimation in policy learning under both missing data settings. Our comprehensive experiments using synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets confirm that correctly specifying the missingness mechanism is crucial: misspecified estimators remain biased regardless of sample size, while our estimators achieve near-oracle performance when assumptions are satisfied. Our work provides practitioners with theoretically grounded, empirically validated tools for robust policy learning in the presence of missing treatment data.

</details>


### [173] [Dysco: Dynamic Subspace Boosting to Mitigate LoRA Interference in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14367)
*Haobo Zhang,Jiankun Wang,Suraj Rajendran,Weishen Pan,Lam Tsoi,Yong Chen,Fei Wang,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic Subspace Boosting (Dysco)，一种解决联邦LoRA微调中数据-参数干扰的插件方法，通过动态分配客户端子空间来稳定聚合。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦联邦学习场景下的LoRA微调稳定性问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然LoRA本身是一种参数高效微调方法，但论文讨论的是联邦场景下的聚合稳定性，而非模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：联邦学习下的LoRA微调稳定性优化。应用场景：联邦微调大语言模型（LLM），实验使用MIMIC-IV临床笔记分类。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）的联邦微调框架，使用Llama-3.2-1B作为基础模型。Dysco作为插件方法，不改变基础模型结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。LoRA本身是一种参数高效微调方法，但论文不讨论模型压缩、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。Dysco关注的是联邦聚合稳定性而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。仅提到Dysco增加0.9%的wall-clock开销。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成联邦任务数据集、MIMIC-IV临床笔记分类数据集。评价指标：合成训练损失（synthetic training loss）、分类准确率（accuracy）。

Experiments: 在合成任务上，Dysco在正交子空间划分下相比基线降低最多9倍训练损失；在MIMIC-IV上，改进所有5种测试的FL算法最多4.3%，优于近期联邦LoRA方法。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景，无法直接迁移；2. 不涉及模型轻量化或边缘部署；3. 实验仅在LLM和临床文本分类上验证，未在视觉/遥感任务上测试；4. 未讨论参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1) LoRA作为参数高效微调方法，在遥感基础模型微调中可降低通信和计算开销，但论文未讨论遥感场景；2) 子空间分配的思想或许可用于遥感多任务学习场景，但需要大幅改造；3) 整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向不匹配，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large pre-trained models increasingly relies on Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to reduce communication and computation, but heterogeneous clients can make adapter aggregation unstable. We identify the data-parameter interference as a geometric source of this instability. This interference is controlled by the alignment between LoRA update subspaces and client activations, suggesting that federated LoRA aggregation should be viewed not only as parameter averaging but also as subspace allocation. We propose Dynamic Subspace Boosting (Dysco), a plug-in method that allocates client-specific LoRA subspaces in a federated and dynamic manner. In each round, clients compute activation-insensitive subspaces from local representations and transmit only the resulting bases; the server then constructs client-specific merged subspaces through a closed-form solution that maximizes compatibility with other clients' insensitive directions. To handle representation drift, Dysco performs multi-round subspace boosting to preserve past update directions while adapting to future representations. We provide a convergence analysis that embeds the data-parameter interference as an aggregation-error term in a standard federated optimization bound, and prove that Dysco's server-fixed merged subspaces yield a tighter upper bound on this error. Experiments on controlled synthetic federated tasks and on MIMIC-IV clinical-note classification with Llama-3.2-1B show that Dysco substantially reduces interference, reduces the final-round synthetic training loss by up to 9 times relative to baselines under the orthogonal-subspace partition the theory identifies, improves all five tested FL algorithms by up to 4.3% on MIMIC, outperforms recent federated LoRA methods, and adds only 0.9% wall-clock overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/illidanlab/Dysco.

</details>


### [174] [A Noise-Robust Elicit-to-Optimize Framework for Distortion Riskmetrics via Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14373)
*Yang Liu,Yuhao Liu,Yunran Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个结合逆强化学习和强化学习的噪声鲁棒框架，用于推断智能体的风险偏好并在扭曲风险度量下优化策略，应用于金融环境。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于金融决策中的风险偏好推断与策略优化，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：风险偏好推断与策略优化；应用场景：金融环境；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构细节。涉及逆强化学习(IRL)、强化学习(RL)、Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)扩展，包含policy网络、value网络和quantile神经网络。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及elicitation accuracy（推断准确性）和effectiveness（有效性），在复杂金融环境中评估。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及"comprehensive empirical study demonstrates the framework's elicitation accuracy and effectiveness in complex financial environments"，未给出具体数值结果或对比方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。该论文属于金融/决策科学领域。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究方向（金融风险偏好推断与策略优化）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全无关。

Abstract: We propose a noise-robust elicit-to-optimize framework that integrates inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and reinforcement learning (RL) for eliciting agents' risk preferences and optimizing policies under a broad class of risk objectives characterized by distortion riskmetrics. On the elicitation side, we propose an adaptive Bayesian IRL method that infers agents' latent risk objectives from their noisy observed decisions, explicitly allowing agents to take stochastic and suboptimal actions. We establish the existence of a finite set of distinguishing questions that identifies the preferred distortion riskmetric within the candidate class and prove that the convergence rate of the algorithm is of order $O(\exp(-cm+O(\sqrt{m\log m})))$ under general settings, where $c>0$ is a constant and $m$ denotes the number of algorithm iterations. On the optimization side, we develop a model-free RL algorithm for optimizing policies under conditional distortion riskmetrics. By representing the objective as an integral of the conditional cost quantile function with respect to the distortion function, the method unifies distortion-riskmetric objectives. We optimize diverse risk objectives by extending the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm with policy, value, and quantile neural networks, where the quantile network estimates the full conditional cost quantile function and enables numerical evaluation of general risk objectives. A comprehensive empirical study demonstrates the framework's elicitation accuracy and effectiveness in complex financial environments.

</details>


### [175] [Integration Matters: Rollout-Based Training for Constrained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14398)
*Xiaoxuan Liang,Saeid Naderiparizi,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过在线rollout获取约束引导并融入训练过程的扩散模型微调框架，以对齐训练与采样过程，改善约束满足度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于扩散模型的约束生成（constrained generation），属于生成式AI/概率建模领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：约束生成（constrained generation），应用场景：扩散模型的可行性约束采样，传感器类型：不涉及，目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于扩散模型（diffusion model），通过固定噪声调度（fixed noise schedule）对去噪过程进行数值积分，具体骨干网络摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提及"across multiple tasks"。

Experiments: 在多个任务上与先前方法对比，所提方法在约束满足度上更优，同时保持有竞争力的采样质量。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 未讨论计算开销和部署可行性；3. 仅针对扩散模型的约束生成问题，与目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果硬要提取思路：其"通过在线rollout对齐训练与推理分布"的思想，在理论上可类比于检测模型训练时模拟部署环境（如量化感知训练QAT、剪枝感知训练）来减小训练-部署差异，但论文本身未朝这个方向展开。

Abstract: Constrained generative models aim to produce samples that satisfy complex feasibility constraints while remaining faithful to the data distribution. Existing constrained generation methods typically enforce constraints either through training-time optimization or sampling-time correction. Training-time optimization approaches optimize on states induced by the training distribution, which can differ substantially from those encountered during sampling. Sampling-time correction methods instead modify the sampling process at inference, introducing distribution shift and requiring expensive tuning, particularly for few-step sampling. We propose a fine-tuning framework that incorporates constraint guidance obtained through online rollout into the training process, which aligns training with sampling by differentiating through the fixed noise schedule used to numerically integrate the denoising process. This exposes the model to violations that arise along the denoising trajectory and aligns diffusion learning with the sampling process. Experiments across multiple tasks show that our method improves constraint satisfaction while maintaining competitive sampling quality compared to prior methods.

</details>


### [176] [LATTICE: Graph Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Spatial Omics Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14410)
*Jagan Mohan Reddy Dwarampudi,Veena Kochat,Suresh Satpati,Kunal Rai,Tania Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LATTICE，一个基于图自监督学习的多模态空间组学数据融合框架，用于整合转录组和表观基因组空间数据。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于生物信息学/空间组学领域，涉及Visium空间转录组、scMultiome、ATAC、CUT&Tag等生物模态数据，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化没有任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态空间组学数据融合与表示学习；应用场景：空间组学（spatial omics），黑色素瘤组织分析；传感器/数据类型：Visium RNA, scMultiome RNA, scMultiome ATAC, spatial ATAC, spatial CUT&Tag；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 基于图的TransformerConv编码器，构建空间邻域图，采用掩码重建、跨模态对齐、空间平滑性三个自监督训练目标。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向生物组学数据分析，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：私有11例黑色素瘤样本队列（54,912个spots）；评价指标：adjusted Rand index (ARI), normalized mutual information (NMI), spatial contiguity, multimodal utility score (MUS)。

Experiments: 与Space Ranger聚类结果对比，加入scMultiome RNA后ARI提升0.157、NMI提升0.143、spatial contiguity提升0.174；更多模态进一步提升spatial contiguity和MUS，但有时降低与RNA参考标签的一致性。

Limitations: 摘要明确提到需要更强的监督信号和更广泛的外部基准测试；使用私有数据集，缺乏公开基准验证；与遥感/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向完全无关，不涉及任何目标检测、遥感图像处理或模型压缩加速技术。

Abstract: Spatially resolved omics studies increasingly combine transcriptomic and epigenomic assays, yet downstream analysis is often still performed using single-modality pipelines. We present LATTICE (Latent Alignment of Tissue-level and Transcriptomic Information for Cross-modal Embedding), a graph-based self-supervised framework that learns spot-level representations from harmonized multimodal features. LATTICE integrates five aligned modality blocks per Visium spot: Visium RNA, scMultiome RNA, scMultiome ATAC, spatial ATAC, and spatial CUT\&Tag. These modalities capture spatial transcriptomic measurements, single-cell inferred regulatory activity, and in situ chromatin and histone states within a unified lattice representation. LATTICE constructs a spatial neighborhood graph and trains a TransformerConv encoder using masked reconstruction, cross-modal alignment, and spatial smoothness objectives. On a private 11-sample melanoma cohort from an anonymized clinical collaborator comprising 54{,}912 total spots, LATTICE demonstrated stable optimization behavior, reproducible embeddings across analysis seeds, and complete multimodal integration across all samples. Adding scMultiome RNA to Visium RNA alone substantially improved concordance with Space Ranger clusters across 11 runs (adjusted Rand index [ARI] +0.157, normalized mutual information [NMI] +0.143, and spatial contiguity +0.174).
