Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴信息。论文本身也声明不包含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名，目前版本规模较小（仅44个病例）。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医疗AI安全评估领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何技术或方法上的关联。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [310] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文评估多模态大语言模型在科学可视化素养测试中的表现，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及任何目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。论文主题是MLLM在科学可视化（SciVis）上的理解能力评估，属于多模态AI与可视化交叉领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态大语言模型科学可视化素养评估；应用场景：科学可视化理解；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 评估了6种MLLM：3个闭源模型（含Gemini）和3个开源模型。摘要未说明具体模型架构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。摘要未说明剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：科学可视化素养评估测试（SciVis literacy assessment test），包含49个题目，基于18个科学可视化图表和插图，涵盖8种可视化技术和11种任务类型。评价指标：与485名人类参与者的数据对比。摘要未说明mAP、FPS、参数量等遥感目标检测常用指标。

Experiments: 主要结果：Gemini整体最强，超过人类均值；开源模型低于人类基线。模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现较好，在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计上表现较差。错误分析显示在细粒度定量估计、流向解释和编码解释方面存在反复失败。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供任何与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化相关的局限性信息。摘要本身未说明其局限性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向完全无关，不涉及遥感、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署中的任何一个核心要素。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [311] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究AI基准测试中项目反应理论(IRT)模型在非标准数据场景下的估计可靠性问题，聚焦LLM基准测试，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是AI基准测试中项目反应理论(IRT)的统计估计方法可靠性，属于AI评估方法论，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：AI基准测试评估方法研究；应用场景：LLM基准测试；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明模型结构。涉及的是IRT统计模型（项目反应理论模型），而非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是IRT估计工具（MML、MCMC、VI、神经伪孪生估计器）的计算可行性，与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：六个广泛使用的LLM基准测试；评价指标：计算可行性、可扩展性、模型排名推断可靠性、预测性能、项目特征推断可靠性。

Experiments: 在18,000个模拟条件下，比较四种IRT估计工具（MML、MCMC、VI、神经伪孪生估计器），评估计算可行性、可扩展性和推断可靠性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于AI评估方法论，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接或间接关联。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [312] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Plover，一种以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过外部化任务计划和重规划为可检查、可修改的工件，提升GUI自动化的透明性和可控性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于GUI自动化（图形用户界面自动化），属于人机交互/智能体领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：GUI自动化（图形用户界面自动化）；应用场景：桌面/移动端界面操作；传感器类型：截图（screenshot）；目标类别：界面元素（按钮、对话框等）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Planner-Executor架构（规划器-执行器架构），具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 基准测试（benchmark）上的失败案例修复评估和基于场景的工作流分析。具体数据集名称和指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 通过基准测试失败案例修复和场景化工作流分析进行评估，结果表明当计划保持可见且干预局部化时，许多GUI智能体失败在结构上是可修复的。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、压缩方法或部署策略。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于人机交互/GUI自动化领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [313] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个结合大语言模型与专家验证的自进化标注框架，用于构建符合DSM-5-TR标准的抑郁症数据集，提升标注一致性和可解释性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于精神健康领域的抑郁症数据集标注质量与可解释AI，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：抑郁症（MDD）数据集标注框架；应用场景：精神健康/临床心理学；传感器类型：不适用（处理文本记录）；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 三阶段标注框架：候选证据选择 → DSM-5-TR标准分析 → 案例级综合标注；双记忆架构（Example Memory + Reflection Memory）用于内化专家反馈。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 抑郁症相关文本数据集；评价指标：标注一致性（annotation consistency）、人工修订工作量（manual revision effort）；使用专家评审样本进行初步实验。

Experiments: 初步实验（pilot study）表明方法提升了标注一致性和可解释性，减少了人工修订工作量。多轮反馈循环的评估留作未来工作。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 多轮反馈循环的评估尚未完成；3. 仅针对抑郁症文本数据，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的研究领域、任务目标、技术路线与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [314] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型(LLM)中文本内容危险(CD)与物理世界危险(PD)的可分离性，并提出基于隐状态探测的PRISM方法用于物理安全检测，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦LLM安全对齐中的物理危险检测问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM物理安全检测（physical safety detection）；应用场景：具身智能体安全对齐；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: PRISM：基于全隐状态的单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针（single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states）。基座模型：Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5, SmolLM2。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：SafeAgentBench, PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K, 自建对比基准, 1000个物理风险对), SafeText, EARBench。评价指标：准确率(Accuracy), 假阳性率(FPR)。

Experiments: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率，11.7-13.7% FPR；同规模LLM法官过阻安全任务达24.7-39.0% FPR。PSB-1K上PRISM达99.6%准确率，0.7% FPR，而Qwen2.5-3B法官拒绝67.8%安全任务。PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上复现结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文研究LLM安全对齐中的物理危险检测，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关技术方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [315] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个用于自动化元分析的多智能体系统AutoSynthesis，实现从文献检索到效应量合并的全流程自动化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于医学/社会科学领域的自动化元分析（meta-analysis），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自动化元分析（automated meta-analysis）；场景：循证医学、教育、政策等领域的证据综合；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 多智能体系统（multi-agent system），包含搜索策略制定、文献检索、研究筛选、全文评估、统计提取、效应量计算、随机效应元分析等模块。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：28篇以上研究文献；评价指标：与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g一致性。

Experiments: 在28篇以上研究上进行筛选，提取20余个定量结论，合并效应量与专家手动元分析结果接近。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于自然语言处理与循证科学的交叉领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [316] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析系统teLLMe，利用因果结构学习、LLM和DoWhy进行交通事件的因果效应估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于城市交通视频数据的因果分析（如天气对交通密度的影响），不涉及遥感卫星、在轨目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：因果分析（causal analysis）；应用场景：城市交通、驾驶安全与拥堵研究；传感器类型：行车记录仪（dashcam）视频；目标类别：交通事件（天气、高峰时段、交通密度等）。不涉及遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Model architecture: 系统组件：PC算法（因果结构学习）、bootstrap稳定性检验、线性回归、DoWhy（因果推断库）、schema-aware LLM（大语言模型）用于自然语言到结构化因果查询的映射。不涉及任何目标检测或轻量化网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上或边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：BDD（Berkeley DeepDrive）衍生的交通事件数据；评价指标：未明确说明，主要依赖因果效应估计、DAG支持度、调整集等因果推断指标。不涉及遥感目标检测常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、SSDD、SAR-AIRcraft-1.0等）。

Experiments: 案例研究：在BDD交通事件数据上展示了系统能够发现天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间的合理因果关系，并显式呈现不确定性和建模选择。无目标检测精度（mAP）、速度（FPS）、参数量等对比实验。

Limitations: 摘要明确说明该系统是用于假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非确定性因果结论的来源。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于因果推断与交通数据分析领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务目标、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [317] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SearchOS，一个面向工具集成大语言模型的多智能体搜索框架，通过显式状态管理解决信息检索中的重复循环和搜索预算浪费问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是大语言模型（LLM）驱动的多智能体信息检索系统，属于自然语言处理/信息检索领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：开放域信息检索（open-domain information seeking）；应用场景：Web搜索、多智能体协作信息获取；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 多智能体框架（multi-agent framework），包含：Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM)、Pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism、Search Tool Middleware Harness、分层技能系统（strategy skills + access skills）。不涉及视觉模型或目标检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：WideSearch、GISA；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称，仅提及"leads all metrics"。

Experiments: 在WideSearch和GISA上，SearchOS在所有评估指标上优于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文聚焦于LLM的搜索效率问题，而非视觉感知或模型压缩问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。但可间接思考：多智能体协作中的"Failure Memory"和"Coverage Map"概念，在分布式星上推理场景中，多个轻量模型节点间的状态共享与失败回退机制或许有类比意义，但关联度极低。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [318] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过公开讨论界面注入恶意数据以污染语言模型预训练语料库的攻击方法，并提出HalfLife分析工具来估计对抗性内容在基于网络爬取的训练数据中的包含情况。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是大语言模型（LM）预训练数据投毒攻击的安全性问题，属于自然语言处理（NLP）和AI安全领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩/部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测等任务。任务类型为语言模型预训练数据投毒攻击分析与检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何视觉模型或目标检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未报告与遥感目标检测或轻量化相关的实验结果。

Limitations: 该论文与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方面的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP安全领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉或可迁移的技术思路。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>

### [319] [OvAi Focus: AI-based Multi-class Segmentation of Functional Ovaries and Adnexal Masses in Gynecological Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14179)
*Niccolò Tallone,Francesca Salis,Pio Raffaele Fina,Roberta Massobrio,Rosilari Bellacosa Marotti,Daniele Conti,Luca Fuso,Luca Mariani,Annamaria Ferrero,Alessandro Arena,Stefano Cosma,Dan Grisaru,Angelo Lacalandra,Renato Seracchioli,Marianna Roccio,Federica Gerace*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一个用于卵巢癌超声图像分割的AI医疗软件设备OvAi Focus，实现功能性卵巢和附件肿块的多类语义分割。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学超声图像分割，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多中心数据集，1,081名成年女性，来自意大利和以色列6个中心；评价指标：DICE系数（完整病灶0.87，囊性0.85，实性0.68，功能性卵巢0.62）。

Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the deadliest gynecological malignancy; accurate and objective segmentation of adnexal masses and functional ovaries in ultrasound (US) remains challenging due to operator variability and morphological complexity. We present OvAi Focus (SynDiag s.r.l., Italy), a stand-alone AI software medical device that performs multi-class semantic segmentation of functional ovaries and adnexal masses, distinguishing cystic from solid components. The system was trained and independently validated on a multicenter dataset of 1,081 adult women from 6 centers across Italy and Israel. Segmentation achieved DICE scores of 0.87 (complete lesion), 0.85 (cystic), 0.68 (solid), and 0.62 (functional ovary), in line with or superior to state-of-the-art approaches across heterogeneous acquisition settings.

</details>

*Yu Zhong,Jingzhi Guo,Luyao Li,Zehao Wang,Zhihui Yang,Yixin Lin,Weilun Fu,Yang Wang*

Model architecture: 离散几何特征提取 + 信息增益特征选择 + Random Forest分类器。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Experiments: 二分类（非严重 vs 严重迂曲）和三分类（直、低迂曲、高迂曲）实验，RF模型在二分类上Macro-F1=0.9206，三分类上Macro-F1=0.8626。基于特征重要性定义了Morphological Risk Index (MRI)。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。医学血管形态分类与遥感目标检测在数据模态、任务目标、部署场景上均无交集。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于医学图像分析中的形态分类任务，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无技术交集。

Abstract: Subjective visual grading of blood vessel tortuosity relies heavily on clinical experience, while traditional distance-based indices often fail to adequately characterize three-dimensional spatial deformation. Because abnormal internal carotid artery morphology may be clinically relevant to cerebrovascular assessment and stroke-risk evaluation, objective and reproducible quantification of vascular tortuosity is of considerable importance. To address this limitation, we propose a mathematical framework for the morphological classification of the internal carotid artery (ICA-C1) segment. The framework integrates discrete geometric feature measurement, Information Gain-based feature selection, and Random Forest classification. An initial set of 13 tortuosity features is extracted from the corresponding 379 clinical vascular centerlines using discrete geometric methods and subsequently reduced to a six-feature subset consisting of $\mathcal{TI}$, $\mathcal{AC}$, $\mathcal{TC}$, $\mathcal{AC}/\mathcal{AT}$, $\mathcal{AT}$, and $\mathcal{TT}$. The framework is evaluated in two classification tasks. For binary classification of non-severe and severe tortuosity, the RF model achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.9206. For ternary morphological grading into straight, low-tortuosity, and high-tortuosity groups, it achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.8626. The results indicate that elongation- and curvature-related features provide strong discriminatory information for basic screening, whereas torsion-related features contribute additional information for more detailed morphological classification. Based on the RF feature-importance values, we further define a Morphological Risk Index (MRI), which provides a direct numerical reference for vascular morphology and may facilitate more objective and consistent clinical assessment.

</details>


### [321] [FORCE-Interior: A Poisson Flow Generative Prior for Interior Tomography Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14320)
*Kang Chen,Wenjun Xia,Jianxu Wang,Mahmud Wasif Nafee,Ge Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FORCE-Interior，一种基于泊松流生成模型的内部断层重建框架，用于解决投影截断导致的ROI重建病态问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学CT（computed tomography）中的内部断层重建（interior tomography），属于医学影像处理领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：内部断层重建（interior tomography reconstruction）；应用场景：医学CT成像；传感器类型：X射线CT投影数据；目标类别：ROI区域重建。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于泊松流生成模型（Poisson-flow generative model）的重建框架，包含全FOV测量约束初始化（full-FOV measurement-constrained initialization）和逐步骤数据一致性约束（per-step data consistency）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向医学CT重建，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及结构重建质量（structural quality）、感知重建质量（perceptual reconstruction quality）和投影域一致性（projection-domain consistency），但未给出具体指标名称（如PSNR、SSIM等）。

Experiments: 在两种更严重截断的ROI尺寸上取得了改进的结构和感知重建质量，在最大ROI尺寸上具有竞争性结果，同时保持了投影域一致性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。该论文解决的是医学CT内部断层重建问题，不涉及目标检测、遥感图像理解或模型压缩部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于医学影像重建领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Interior tomography reconstructs a region of interest (ROI) from truncated projection measurements. However, projection truncation makes the inverse problem severely ill-posed, leading to non-unique solutions, oversmoothing, and truncation-induced artifacts when conventional reconstruction methods are directly applied to interior tomography. Existing learning-based interior CT methods have shown promising performance, but their generalization across different truncation patterns, ROI sizes, and noise levels remains an important challenge. Meanwhile, current generative model-based reconstruction methods are primarily designed for non-interior tomography settings and do not directly address ROI-based projection truncation. Moreover, without sufficient data-consistency constraints, generative sampling may yield anatomically plausible but measurement-inconsistent structures. To address these challenges, we propose FORCE-Interior, a Poisson-flow generative reconstruction framework for interior tomography. FORCE-Interior combines a full-FOV measurement-constrained initialization with per-step data consistency for ROI-truncated measurements, anchoring generative sampling to the acquired measurements throughout reconstruction. Experiments show that FORCE-Interior achieves improved structural and perceptual reconstruction quality at the two more severely truncated ROI sizes, with competitive results at the largest, while maintaining projection-domain consistency.

</details>


### [322] [ViPSAM: Visual Prompting Medical Image Segmentation Using Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14328)
*San Lee,Nalee Kim,Jeong Il Yu,Hee Chul Park,Boah Kim*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ViPSAM，一种基于视觉提示的跨模态分割框架，利用增强MRI辅助非增强CT的肝脏病灶分割，基于SAM模型进行参数高效微调。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于医学影像（质子治疗中的肝脏病灶分割），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等模型压缩或加速方法。唯一可能相关的点是使用了SAM作为基础模型，但应用场景和任务目标与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对跨模态视觉提示或SAM微调技术本身有泛化兴趣，否则无需阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像分割（病灶分割）；应用场景：质子治疗规划中的非增强CT肝脏病灶分割；传感器类型：非增强CT（NCCT）和增强MRI；目标类别：肝脏病灶（lesion）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于SAM（Segment Anything Model）构建，引入视觉提示编码器（visual prompt encoder）提取增强图像的引导特征，以及视觉引导交叉注意力模块（visual-guided cross-attention module）融合非增强与增强特征，掩码解码器采用参数高效方式适配视觉提示。摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs等。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。仅提到掩码解码器以"parameter-efficient manner"适配，但这是针对微调效率而非模型轻量化或推理加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该论文面向医学影像离线处理场景，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：质子治疗中获取的肝脏病灶NCCT数据（摘要未说明具体规模、来源）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称，仅提及"outperforms representative U-Net- and SAM-based methods"。

Experiments: 与U-Net和SAM-based方法对比，ViPSAM在非增强CT肝脏病灶分割上表现更优。摘要未提供定量结果（如Dice、IoU等数值）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对医学影像分割任务，方法设计高度依赖跨模态（CT+MRI）临床场景，难以直接迁移到遥感目标检测；3. 未提供任何轻量化或部署相关信息；4. 基于SAM的框架计算开销大，不适合在轨实时推理。

Ideas for my research: 1. 视觉提示（visual prompting）和跨模态注意力机制的设计思路，在遥感领域可能用于多模态融合（如光学+SAR、多光谱+高光谱）目标检测；2. 参数高效微调（parameter-efficient fine-tuning）策略可借鉴用于遥感预训练模型（如遥感SAM变体）的轻量化适配；3. 但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: In proton therapy planning, respiratory-gated non-contrast CT (NCCT) is commonly used for lesion segmentation; however, accurate delineation remains challenging due to low lesion-to-background contrast. Although learning-based methods have shown strong performance, they often struggle with non-contrast image segmentation. Inspired by clinical practice, where contrast-enhanced MRI is referenced to delineate lesions on NCCT, we propose ViPSAM, a visual prompting framework that leverages complementary cross-modality information. Built upon the Segment Anything Model (SAM), ViPSAM introduces a visual prompt encoder to extract guidance features from contrast-enhanced images and a visual-guided cross-attention module to integrate non-contrast and contrast-enhanced features, thereby enhancing lesion-relevant representations in low-contrast regions. The mask decoder is further adapted in a parameter-efficient manner to utilize visual prompts effectively. We evaluate the proposed method on liver lesion segmentation using NCCT acquired for proton therapy. Experimental results demonstrate that ViPSAM outperforms representative U-Net- and SAM-based methods, indicating that cross-modality visual prompting enables more robust and accurate segmentation in non-contrast images.

</details>


### [323] [Domain Adaptation of Mismatched Proximal Denoiser for Plug-and-Play Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14894)
*Guixian Xu,Jinglai Li,Junqi Tang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出"近端失配"(proximal mismatch)概念，分析PnP-PGD图像重建方法在去噪器域外部署时的收敛性，并提出基于近端匹配的域适应策略。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图像重建（deblurring, super-resolution）的PnP-PGD算法理论分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何一项。虽然"域外部署"概念与在轨部署中的域偏移有表面相似性，但方法本身是图像重建领域的理论分析，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像重建（Gaussian deblurring, super-resolution）；应用场景：通用图像恢复；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: PnP-PGD（Plug-and-Play Proximal Gradient Descent）框架，使用去噪器作为隐式先验。涉及两类去噪器族：learned proximal networks 和 gradient-step denoisers。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验任务：Gaussian deblurring 和 super-resolution；域偏移设置：substantial domain shift；摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明：在域偏移下，proximal matching adaptation 相比 MSE-based adaptation 显著提升重建质量，在few-shot场景下增益最大。收敛性方面：推导出以O(1/K)速率衰减的stationarity bound，附加项与平均平方proximal mismatch成正比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对图像重建任务，不涉及检测任务；3. 未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法；4. 未提供部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发点：论文讨论的"域外部署"问题与遥感模型在轨部署时面临的训练-测试域偏移（如不同传感器、不同光照条件）有概念上的相似性，但方法本身（proximal matching adaptation）是针对图像重建的PnP框架，难以直接迁移到目标检测模型的轻量化场景。

Abstract: Plug-and-play proximal gradient descent (PnP-PGD) enables flexible image reconstruction by using denoisers as implicit priors. In practice, these denoisers are often deployed outside their training domains. Existing analyses establish convergence under structural assumptions on the deployed denoiser, such as requiring it to be a proximal map or a contraction. However, they do not measure how domain mismatch affects convergence of PnP-PGD. We define this effect as \emph{proximal mismatch}: the discrepancy between a deployed denoiser $\widehat{\mathsf D}$ and a target-domain reference map $\mathsf D_\star=\operatorname{prox}_{R_\star}$ associated with the underlying regularizer $R_\star$. Under this mismatch, each denoising update becomes an inexact proximal step for the target objective. We further derive a stationarity bound that decays at a rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/K)$, with an additive term proportional to the average squared proximal mismatch. This result motivates adaptation via proximal matching rather than MSE-based adaptation alone. We study this approach with two established denoiser families: learned proximal networks and gradient-step denoisers. Experiments on Gaussian deblurring and super-resolution under substantial domain shift show that proximal matching adaptation improves reconstruction quality significantly over MSE-based adaptation, yielding the largest numerical gains in the few-shot regime.

</details>


### [324] [Deep Scene-Driven Ordering of Hadamard Basis for Single-Pixel Spectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15045)
*Brayan Monroy,Hans Garcia,Henry Arguello,Jorge Bacca*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种场景驱动的Hadamard基序贯优化方法，用于改进单像素成像（SPI）中深度光学编码设计（DOCD）的光谱图像重建质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于光谱图像获取硬件（单像素成像+光学编码设计），属于计算成像/光学传感领域，不涉及遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：光谱图像重建（spectral image reconstruction）；场景：环境监测、精准农业；传感器：单像素成像（SPI）系统；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明具体遥感平台或卫星场景。

Model architecture: 基于深度光学编码设计（DOCD）的端到端优化框架，核心是场景驱动的Hadamard基序贯选择机制，用于SPI调制模式生成。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 光谱数据集（spectral datasets）及真实测试台采集（real test-bed acquisitions）。评价指标：图像质量（improving quality of VIS and NIR spectral images），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在光谱数据集仿真和真实测试台采集上验证，与固定设计（fixed designs）对比，所提方法在可见光（VIS）和近红外（NIR）光谱图像质量上均有提升。摘要未提供定量对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算开销和实时性；3. 仅涉及光谱图像获取环节，不涉及后续检测/识别任务；4. 未说明在轨或边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：场景驱动的自适应采样策略（scene-driven ordering）思想，或许可类比于遥感目标检测中根据场景复杂度动态调整推理策略（如动态分辨率/动态网络），但这属于非常间接的类比。

Abstract: Spectral images are highly valuable for various applications, including environmental monitoring and precision agriculture. However, the high cost of specialized sensors limits the wide use of this technology in numerous applications. Current alternatives to acquire high spatial-spectral resolution spectral images, like Single-Pixel Imaging (SPI) enhanced with Deep Optical Coding Design (DOCD), have limitations due to their non-feedback optical designs, leading to limited image quality, with optimal performance achieved only for the specific scenes used during training. This work reformulates the DOCD framework to handle the scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard basis within the SPI architecture for spectral imaging. Taking into account that SPI usually acquires hundreds of snapshots, our approach introduces a scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard matrix for flexible SPI modulation pattern selection based on scene characteristics in an end-to-end optimization. Simulations on spectral datasets and real test-bed acquisitions demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in improving the quality of VIS and NIR spectral images compared to fixed designs.

</details>


### [325] [ESAR: Event-Based Synthetic Aperture Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15073)
*Harbir Antil,Daniel Blauvelt,David Sayre*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于事件相机的单目成像方法，将事件流建模为合成孔径逆问题，通过正则化反演恢复静态场景的对数辐射场，在近天底方向事件数据上验证了恢复大尺度空间结构的能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文涉及事件相机成像（event-based imaging），非传统光学遥感或SAR/高光谱/多光谱遥感图像；不涉及目标检测；不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。虽然使用了Falcon Neuro事件数据（可能来自卫星平台），但核心问题是事件流重建静态场景，而非遥感目标检测或轻量化推理。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不推荐精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：单目事件相机成像（monocular event-based imaging），合成孔径逆问题求解。应用场景：近天底方向（near-nadir）事件数据重建。传感器类型：事件相机（event camera）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，关注的是场景辐射场重建。摘要未说明具体遥感应用场景。

Model architecture: 核心方法：将事件流建模为线性化模型 APθ = b + η，其中θ为静态场景对数辐射场，P为几何投影映射，A为时间差分算子。通过正则化反演（regularized inversion）恢复θ。不是深度学习模型，而是基于物理模型的逆问题求解方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。方法基于正则化反演求解，计算复杂度取决于网格规模Ng，但未讨论实时性或星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：模拟数据（simulated data）和真实近天底Falcon Neuro事件数据（real near-nadir Falcon Neuro event data）。评价指标：摘要未说明具体定量指标，仅定性描述"recovers coherent large-scale spatial structure"。对比基线：动态潜像方法（dynamic latent-image baselines）和学习型事件重建方法（learned event-reconstruction baselines）。

Experiments: 数值实验表明，所提出的θ-based公式相比动态潜像和学习型事件重建基线，能恢复连贯的大尺度空间结构，同时抑制细尺度纹理。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 方法仅恢复大尺度空间结构，抑制细尺度纹理，意味着空间分辨率有限，不适合小目标检测。2. 仅在近天底方向数据上验证，对非天底视角或复杂运动场景的泛化性未知。3. 未与遥感目标检测任务结合，未评估对检测性能的影响。4. 计算效率未讨论，正则化反演在大规模网格上可能计算代价高。5. 事件相机在遥感领域的实际部署尚不成熟。

Ideas for my research: 1. 弱相关：事件相机作为一种新型传感器，其高时间分辨率（微秒级）特性可能对在轨运动目标检测有潜在价值，但本文聚焦于静态场景重建而非检测。2. 合成孔径逆问题的建模思路（将多帧观测统一到公共辐射场）可类比遥感中的超分辨率重建或去模糊任务，但与轻量化目标检测方向距离较远。3. 不建议作为本课题的核心参考论文。

Abstract: Event cameras report asynchronous polarity events when changes in log--radiance exceed a fixed contrast threshold, producing signed temporal contrast measurements rather than conventional image frames. We formulate monocular event-based imaging as a synthetic-aperture inverse problem for a static ground-domain log--radiance field $θ\in \mathbb{R}^{N_g}$. Instead of reconstructing a latent pixel-time volume $v \in \mathbb{R}^{N_pN_t}$, we impose the geometric relation $v=Pθ$, where $P$ maps the fixed scene into motion-dependent latent views. Aggregating events over finite time intervals gives the linearized model \[
  APθ= b+η, \] where $A$ is a temporal differencing operator, $b$ contains signed binned event counts, and $η$ represents measurement and modeling errors. This decomposition exposes a synthetic-aperture structure: under near-nadir motion, successive projections are approximately shifted views of a common scene, while the composite operator $AP$ remains ill-conditioned because it combines spatial averaging with temporal differencing. We therefore use regularized inversion to recover $θ$. Numerical experiments on simulated data and real near-nadir Falcon Neuro event data show that the proposed $θ$-based formulation recovers coherent large-scale spatial structure, relative to dynamic latent-image and learned event-reconstruction baselines, while suppressing fine-scale texture.

</details>

### [326] [Generalized Neural Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14122)
*Natan Hilario da Silva,Vicente Garibay Cancho,Adriano Kamimura Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出GNDR框架，将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，实现不确定性量化，应用于临床计数、生存分析和面部图像年龄分布估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于概率分布回归与不确定性量化，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：概率分布回归与不确定性量化；应用场景：临床计数、转录组生存分析、面部图像年龄估计；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，上游网络作为固定非线性基展开，终端预测头隔离处理。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：临床计数数据、转录组生存数据、面部图像年龄数据；评价指标：分布校准（distributional calibration），具体指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在过离散临床计数、右删失混合治愈生存分析、零截断面部图像年龄分布三种数据模态上验证了框架的通用性和优越的分布校准性能。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与课题方向无关，无直接可借鉴点。其不确定性量化方法（Fisher信息矩阵、Delta方法）若未来应用于遥感检测的不确定性估计，可能有一定间接参考价值，但摘要中未涉及遥感场景。

Abstract: We introduce the Generalized Neural Distributional Regression (GNDR) framework, which seamlessly embeds deep neural networks into the parameter space of classical probability distributions. To reconcile the inherent non-identifiability of deep architectures with maximum likelihood theory, we propose a two-step semi-parametric estimation procedure. By isolating the terminal prediction heads and treating the upstream network as a fixed, non-linear basis expansion, GNDR enables the extraction of analytical Fisher Information matrices. This facilitates rigorous uncertainty quantification, generating observation-specific confidence bands and tolerance intervals via the multivariate Delta method. We demonstrate the framework's versatility and superior distributional calibration across diverse data modalities, including overdispersed clinical counts, right-censored transcriptomic survival profiles under a mixture cure framework, and zero-truncated age distributions derived directly from unstructured facial images. The methodology is natively implemented in the open-source Python package \textit{thetaflow}.

</details>


### [327] [Operator-Informed Gaussian Processes for Complex Helmholtz Wavefields: From Synthetic Benchmarks to In Vivo Brain Elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14193)
*Boyuan Deng,Kshitiz Upadhyay,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文将算子条件高斯过程回归扩展到复值Helmholtz方程，用于耗散介质中的波场推断，并返回后验不确定性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是物理信息驱动的波传播方程求解（Helmholtz方程），应用于脑磁共振弹性成像，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：复值波场推断（Helmholtz方程求解）；应用场景：耗散介质中的波传播，in vivo脑磁共振弹性成像；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 算子条件高斯过程回归（operator-informed GP regression），通过将复值算子实部化（realifying）为等价的耦合实值块，使用标准实值GP条件进行推断。支持对角先验、coregionalized和多尺度变体。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 基准问题：一维到三维的Helmholtz方程基准测试；实际应用：in vivo脑磁共振弹性成像数据。评价指标：与有限差分和神经网络基线的对比、与测量的相关性（correlation，目标0.75，达到0.77）。

Experiments: 在一维到三维基准问题上，该方法在远更少的内部约束点下与有限差分和神经网络基线竞争；在脑磁共振弹性成像中，多尺度先验重建剪切旋度场与测量的相关性达到0.77，超过0.75目标。增益来自多尺度核而非实-虚耦合。

Limitations: 存在低频精度上限（由模型不匹配导致）；后验不确定性尚未校准（not yet calibrated），校准不确定性被作者指出为耗散介质中概率波场推断的核心下一步。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。唯一可提及的是其不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）思路，但应用场景和方法论差异极大，不具迁移性。

Abstract: The Helmholtz equation governs time-harmonic wave propagation, and in dissipative media a complex modulus renders its squared wavenumber $κ^2$ complex. Inferring such fields from sparse, noisy data calls for solvers that also quantify their own uncertainty. Physics-informed Gaussian-process (GP) regression supplies this by returning a posterior over the solution, yet operator-conditioned formulations have been developed almost exclusively for real-valued fields. We extend operator-informed GP regression to complex-valued Helmholtz problems by realifying the complex operator into an equivalent coupled real block, which enables inference with standard real-valued GP conditioning. The construction admits a family of priors, from a proper diagonal prior to coregionalized and multiscale variants, and conditions on PDE residuals and boundary traces. On benchmark problems in one to three dimensions, the solver is competitive with finite-difference and neural-network baselines at a far smaller interior-constraint budget. Unlike those deterministic baselines, it returns a posterior over the complex wavefield rather than a point estimate. Applied to \textit{in vivo} brain magnetic resonance elastography, a proper multiscale prior reconstructs the shear curl field to a correlation of $0.77$ with measurement, above a $0.75$ target. The gain arises from the multiscale kernel rather than from real--imaginary coupling. We further identify a low-frequency accuracy ceiling set by model mismatch and a posterior uncertainty that is not yet calibrated. Calibrated uncertainty therefore emerges as the central next step for probabilistic wavefield inference in dissipative media.

</details>


### [328] [Spectral Concentration and Recovery in Sparse High-Dimensional Random Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14304)
*Manuel Fernandez,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维随机几何图的谱性质与潜向量恢复，属于随机图论与高维统计的理论工作，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于纯数学/理论计算机科学方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: We study sparse random geometric graphs generated by connecting pairs of high-dimensional vectors whose inner product exceeds a threshold. The latent vectors are sampled either uniformly from the sphere or from a standard Gaussian distribution. Although every edge appears with probability $p$, the edges are dependent through their shared latent vectors. For the spherical model, at the connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, we prove $\|A-\mathbb E A\|=O\left(\sqrt{np\log n}+npτ\right)$, with high probability, where $τ$ is the cap threshold. This sharpens the spectral norm bound of Liu, Mohanty, Schramm, and Yang (2023) under weaker assumptions. An analogous result holds for the Gaussian model after removing the fluctuations of the vector norms, yielding improved global synchronization guarantees for the homogeneous Kuramoto model. We then recover the latent geometry from the leading eigenspace. When $np\gg\log n$, both the latent vector and relative Gram matrix errors vanish provided $d\ll np\log(1/p)/\log n$. The required lower dimension is only $d\gg\log(1/p)$ for the spherical model and $d\gg\log^2(1/p)\log n$ for the Gaussian model, improving the recovery guarantees of Li and Schramm (2023). Finally, we prove the first exact recovery result for the Gaussian mixture block model of Li and Schramm (2023). At the optimal connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, a polynomial-time semidefinite program exactly recovers all labels in a moderate-separation regime, whereas larger separation makes exact recovery impossible because isolated vertices appear with high probability. Our proofs combine orthogonal polynomial expansions, decoupling, and matrix concentration, avoiding the trace-moment arguments used in previous work.

</details>


### [329] [Optimal Self-Distillation for Rectified Flow via Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14947)
*Saptarshi Roy,Debepsita Mukherjee,Pratik Patil*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究最优自蒸馏方法用于修正rectified flow中的次优教师速度场，通过理论推导最优混合系数并证明可提升生成质量。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于生成模型（rectified flow）中的自蒸馏理论分析，属于生成式AI/扩散模型的理论研究，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生成模型中的自蒸馏（self-distillation）理论分析；应用场景：生成式建模（图像生成）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Rectified flow（RF）模型，线性RF带ridge正则化，固定插值对。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏（self-distillation），但蒸馏目的是提升生成质量而非模型压缩或加速。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验使用：Gaussian模型、Gaussian混合模型、图像数据。评价指标：velocity risk、mode recovery、finite-step generation质量。

Experiments: 通过理论证明和实验验证：最优自蒸馏可改善velocity risk、mode recovery和有限步生成质量，优于教师模型和纯蒸馏方法。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化部署完全无关；2. 理论分析限于线性RF和ridge正则化场景，泛化性有限；3. 不涉及任何遥感数据集或检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可注意：论文中的自蒸馏理论（最优混合系数、sign rule）若未来迁移到遥感检测模型的蒸馏压缩场景，可能提供理论指导——例如在知识蒸馏中如何最优地混合教师和学生信号。不过当前论文完全面向生成模型，直接迁移需大量适配工作。

Abstract: Modern generative models are increasingly trained using model-generated signals, creating both opportunities for self-improvement and risks of collapse. We study optimal self-distillation (SD) for rectified flow (RF): given a suboptimal teacher velocity field, can a student trained on a mixture of true RF velocities and teacher velocities provably improve the teacher? For linear RF with ridge regularization on fixed interpolation pairs, we prove an exact affine path identity, derive the optimal mixing coefficient in closed form, and show strict improvement in integrated velocity risk whenever the teacher risk is nonstationary along the regularization path. The optimal coefficient obeys a sign rule: positive mixing corrects under-regularized teachers, while negative mixing corrects over-regularized teachers. We also give one-shot generalized cross-validation (GCV) and validation tuning procedure that avoids grid search over mixing weights and repeated refitting. Combining this theorem with RF Wasserstein convergence bounds, we show that optimal self-distillation improves the velocity estimation terms controlling continuous-time and finite-step generation error. Experiments with Gaussian models, Gaussian mixtures, and image data show that optimal self-distillation improves velocity risk, mode recovery, and finite-step generation relative to both the teacher and pure distillation.

</details>


### [330] [cGAP: Generalized Association Plots with HOMALS-Guided Heatmaps for Visualization of High-Dimensional Categorical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15018)
*Chun-houh Chen,Shun-Chuan Chang,Chiun-How Kao,Yi-Ju Lee,Shang-Ying Shiu,Yin-Jing Tien,ShengLi Tzeng,Han-Ming Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出cGAP框架，用于高维分类数据的可视化分析，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是高维分类数据的可视化方法（cGAP），应用领域为遗传学、生物医学、社会科学等，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及任何目标检测任务或模型轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：分类数据的可视化分析；场景：遗传学、生物医学、社会科学；数据类型：名义型、有序型、二值型分类数据；完全不涉及遥感图像或目标检测。

Model architecture: cGAP框架基于Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS)进行三维嵌入，映射为RGB颜色，集成三个视图：HOMALS引导的热图、样本邻近矩阵、变量邻近矩阵，并使用Seriation算法重排行/列。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作不面向星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：学生-动物分类数据、哺乳动物齿列数据、UCI蘑菇数据集、COG数据库。评价指标：未明确给出定量指标，主要通过barycentric traceability、projection-distortion、contrast-preservation等性质进行定性评估。

Experiments: 在四个分类数据集上进行了可视化展示，展示了聚类、异常值和局部到全局结构的发现能力。无对比方法、无消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于高维分类数据可视化领域，与遥感图像目标检测、模型轻量化、星上部署等研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: High-dimensional categorical data arise in genetics, biomedicine, and the social sciences, yet visualization tools for such data remain far less developed than those for continuous variables. Existing methods either scale poorly, rely heavily on low-dimensional displays detached from the original data matrix, or prioritize predictive accuracy over interpretability. To address this gap, we introduce categorical Generalized Association Plots (cGAP), a visualization framework for nominal, ordinal, and binary data that preserves the original data matrix while augmenting it with interpretable geometric structure. cGAP uses Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS) to embed subjects and category levels in a three-dimensional Euclidean space and maps the embedding to red-green-blue coordinates so that similar patterns receive similar colors. The framework integrates three coordinated views: a HOMALS-guided heatmap of the raw data matrix, a subject proximity matrix, and a variable proximity matrix. Seriation algorithms are then used to reorder rows and columns to reveal coherent clusters, outliers, and local-to-global structure. We also derive barycentric traceability, projection-distortion, and contrast-preservation properties that clarify how embedding geometry is transferred to the display. We demonstrate the versatility of cGAP through applications to student-animal classification data, mammalian dentition profiles, mushroom records from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, and the Clusters of Orthologous Genes database. These examples show that cGAP supports transparent exploratory analysis by maintaining traceability between derived visual structure and the original categorical observations. cGAP provides a full-matrix, heatmap-based visualization environment for investigating complex categorical datasets across scientific domains.

</details>


### [331] [Subjective Risk Decomposition: A New View for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15196)
*Raghad Alamri,Michele Caprio,Gavin Brown*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于主观风险分解的不确定性量化新视角，将认知不确定性和偶然不确定性视为高阶建模决策的衍生结果，而非原始度量。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于纯机器学习理论中的不确定性量化（UQ）研究，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是通用不确定性量化理论，未涉及任何遥感、卫星、目标检测等具体任务或场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未提出任何具体的神经网络模型架构，而是从理论层面讨论不确定性度量的推导框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文完全不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及任何实验数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未提供任何实验对比或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 该论文是纯理论工作，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴的工程或方法贡献。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然该论文本身不涉及遥感或轻量化，但其不确定性量化理论框架（主观风险分解、严格适当损失函数）可能在以下方面有间接启发：（1）在轨目标检测中，对检测结果的不确定性估计可用于决策可靠性评估；（2）轻量化模型往往精度下降，不确定性量化可帮助判断哪些预测不可信。但需要自行将理论映射到遥感检测场景，且论文未提供任何工程实现细节。

Abstract: We present a novel viewpoint for uncertainty quantification. Uncertainty measures are not primitives, in need of axioms and argumentation, but instead consequences, of higher-level modelling decisions. We show how epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty measures can be derived via decomposition of a subjective risk, based on a strictly proper loss. Reverse cross-entropy provides a prominent example, where decomposition recovers the classic information-theoretic uncertainty terms. The same approach recovers numerous measures previously proposed across the UQ literature, providing them a common theoretical foundation. From a practical point of view, this suggests a new approach to UQ: given a modelling scenario and strictly proper loss, the corresponding epistemic and aleatoric terms are induced by the subjective-risk decomposition. We then extend our view to learning theory: we introduce and analyse subjective risk analogues of excess risk, approximation error, and estimation error, and identify the connections to UQ. We consider this a first step towards a full learning-theoretic framework for uncertainty quantification.

</details>
